# photo-manager — Feature Inventory

Canonical catalogue of user-visible behaviour. Each section names one
feature: where it is reachable, what the user does to trigger it, what
happens, and which conditions change the result.

This file is the answer to "is this behaviour documented?" and
"what's the expected UX for X?". It complements — does not replace —
the happy-path walkthrough in [`README.md` § Usage](../README.md#usage).

The full backfill ships in two stages: [PR #263](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/263)
seeded the Execute Action cluster; a later PR completed the inventory
(scan, review, save/load, preview, i18n). `docs_guard` now requires an
update here whenever user-visible behaviour changes — see
[#262](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/262).

> **Reading this after the #646 cutover.** Every entry describes the shipped
> web client (FastAPI backend + React frontend);
> [#888](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/888) rewrote the
> entries that still described the removed desktop client. Headings without a
> `Web —` prefix predate the port and keep their names so in-page links stay
> stable. An entry whose behaviour did not survive the port says **removed
> with the desktop client in #646** in its Entry point. Any other mention of
> the desktop client is history — where a behaviour, a decision or a
> divergence came from — never a description of current behaviour.

### Bulk regex — remove from list (deferred decision)

- **Entry point:** The Set Action dialog's action combo (`action-action-combo`, [frontend/src/components/action/ActionDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/action/ActionDialog.tsx)) — its **Skip** option (catalog key `web.decision.remove_from_list`, wire value `__ignore__`). The dialog opens from the toolbar's **Set Action…**, the menu bar's **Action → Set Action by Field…**, or a row's right-click **Set Action by Field…** — see [Web — Action dialog (Set Action by Field)](#web--action-dialog-set-action-by-field).
- **Trigger:** Build a match (field + pattern), pick **Skip** as the action, click **Apply**.
- **Behaviour:** Apply sends `POST /api/action/bulk-decide` with `action="__ignore__"`; the backend maps it to `user_decision='ignore'` on every matched row. Nothing is moved or deleted and no row leaves the tree yet: each matched row keeps `outcome=''` and shows **Skip** selected in its decision control. Unlike **Delete**, Skip applies with no confirm sheet. The removal happens at **Execute Action**: the execute service collects every in-review row decided `ignore` alongside the delete rows, finalizes the ignore rows to `outcome='ignored'` in one batch (files untouched on disk), and they drop out of the review because the manifest loads only rows `WHERE outcome=''` (the [#584](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/584) unification). Until then the Execute dialog lists them next to the delete rows, and its type filter can isolate them.
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - Matched rows that are locked make Apply answer 409 `locked_paths`, which opens the dialog's inline lock-confirm (Cancel / Apply to Unlocked Only / Unlock & Apply) — see [Web — Action dialog (Set Action by Field)](#web--action-dialog-set-action-by-field).
  - The same reversible, deferred Skip is on every row's decision control (the **Skip** segment).
  - The toolbar's bulk **Skip** verb and the preview pane's **Skip** button stage the same reversible decision. The remaining Skip entries **finalize on the spot** instead of deferring: the main-tree right-click **Skip now — drop from this review** (file rows: the selection-aware target set; group header: the group's members) and the menu bar's **List → Skip Selected now**, both with no confirm ([#694](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/694)); and the Execute dialog's row-menu **Skip — leave on disk, drop from this review**, which finalizes that one row behind a confirm ("Skip this file?" — Cancel / Yes, skip it). The "now" in the two main-window labels is what marks them as immediate.
- **Related:** [PR #158](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/158); [#584](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/584); [#694](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/694); [core/app_service/action_service.py](../core/app_service/action_service.py) (`bulk_decide`, `__ignore__` → `ignore`); [core/app_service/execute_service.py](../core/app_service/execute_service.py) (the ignore batch at execute; `remove_from_review` for the immediate paths); unit tests [`tests/test_web_action_routes.py`](../tests/test_web_action_routes.py) (`test_top1_desc_sets_decision_on_highest_score_per_group` writes `ignore` via `__ignore__`) and [`tests/test_web_execute_routes.py`](../tests/test_web_execute_routes.py) (`test_ignore_decided_files_get_outcome_ignored`); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s29_remove_from_list_by_regex.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s29_remove_from_list_by_regex.py) (the deferred bulk path — matched rows carry `ignore`, the dialog closes with no confirm), [`qa/web/scenarios/s20_multi_remove_from_list.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s20_multi_remove_from_list.py) (main-tree multi-selection Skip now finalizes), [`qa/web/scenarios/s54_execute_dialog_remove_from_list.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s54_execute_dialog_remove_from_list.py) (the Execute dialog's single-row Skip behind its confirm).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Context menu — Lock / Unlock

- **Entry point:** The file-row right-click menu of the main result tree ([frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx)) shows **Lock** (`ctx-lock`) or **Unlock** (`ctx-unlock`) according to the right-clicked row's current state. The same flag is reachable from each row's padlock in the Lock column (`row-lock-{group}-{name}`, [frontend/src/components/result/LockToggle.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/LockToggle.tsx)), from the Execute dialog's row menu ([frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx)), and from the Set Action dialog's **Lock** / **Unlock** actions (wire values `__lock__` / `__unlock__`).
- **Trigger:** Right-click a file row (single, or part of a multi-selection) and pick **Lock** or **Unlock**.
- **Behaviour:** Sets the `is_locked` flag — orthogonal to `user_decision`, which it leaves alone — on every target row (the whole multi-selection when the right-clicked row is in it, otherwise just that row) in one `PATCH /api/lock`, applied optimistically and reverted if the request fails. A locked row shows a solid accent padlock (a faint open one when unlocked), its decision control is disabled, and it is frozen against the writes that would change or act on its decision: a decision write (`PATCH /api/decision`, a Set Action bulk apply, the right-click Apply best-copy), **Skip now**, and Execute of a row decided **Delete** each answer 409 `locked_paths`, which opens the lock-confirm dialog — see [Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog). The group header's **Keep best · delete rest** button instead leaves locked rows untouched.
- **Conditions / variants:** Lock and Unlock never open the lock-confirm dialog themselves — `PATCH /api/lock` has no lock gate and the Set Action dialog's lock/unlock actions bypass it — so they stay the escape valve for the freeze. The group-header menu has no Lock/Unlock. In the Execute dialog's row menu they act on the right-clicked row only.
- **Related:** [PR #175](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/175) (closes [#164](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/164)); selection behaviour under [Web — main result tree multi-selection](#web--main-result-tree-multi-selection); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s35_lock_via_context_menu.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s35_lock_via_context_menu.py) (Lock → Unlock round trip on one row and then two rows, `is_locked` read back via `GET /api/manifest`) and [`qa/web/scenarios/s53_execute_dialog_lock_decision.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s53_execute_dialog_lock_decision.py) (the Execute-dialog route, plus a locked row's decision write answering 409); unit [`tests/test_web_review_routes.py`](../tests/test_web_review_routes.py) (`test_lock_returns_updated_count`, `test_unlock_also_works`, `test_locked_row_without_flags_returns_409`).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-07-22 (web-docs-drift backfill — added the missing web pointer)

### Context menu — Open Folder

- **Entry point:** The file-row right-click menu of the main result tree — **Open folder** (`ctx-open-folder`, [frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx)). It is not on the group-header menu or on the Execute dialog's row menu.
- **Trigger:** Right-click a file row and pick **Open folder**.
- **Behaviour:** Targets only the right-clicked row, even inside a multi-selection. `store.revealInExplorer` posts `POST /api/reveal {file_path}`; the server accepts it only from localhost and only for a path under the allowed roots (the scanned folders), then launches `explorer /select,<path>` fire-and-forget, so Explorer opens the containing folder with the file pre-selected. Windows only: on any other platform the endpoint answers 501 `platform_unsupported`. A failed reveal is non-fatal — the manifest is untouched and the error is written to `execute.executeError`, which the footer shows as its `main-execute-error` line ("Action error: …"; a failed Explorer launch reads "The file manager could not be opened.") while the Execute dialog is closed; with that dialog open, the dialog shows the error itself. See [Main window — status bar baseline](#main-window--status-bar-baseline) for the footer's error lines.
- **Conditions / variants:** The full-resolution viewer's load-error fallback button (**Open in default app**) calls the same reveal. Double-clicking a row does not reveal it — that opens the in-app viewer (see [Main window — results tree double-click](#main-window--results-tree-double-click)).
- **Related:** Originally part of the [PR #19](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/19) context-menu redesign; extracted into a shared helper in [PR #198](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/198). QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s19_context_menu_open_folder.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s19_context_menu_open_folder.py) (the item fires a `POST /api/reveal` carrying the right `file_path` — the driver captures the first such request; 200 on Windows, 501 elsewhere); unit [`tests/test_web_execute_routes.py`](../tests/test_web_execute_routes.py) (`TestPostReveal`: non-localhost 403, non-Windows 501, Windows launches Explorer once), [`frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.test.tsx`](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.test.tsx) (Open folder calls `revealInExplorer`), [`frontend/src/App.test.tsx`](../frontend/src/App.test.tsx) (the `main-execute-error` footer line and its suppression while the Execute dialog is open).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-07-16 (web audit remediation — `MAIN_EXECUTE_ERROR` footer surface for failed reveal)

### Context menu — Set Action (per-file)

- **Entry point:** The file-row right-click menu of the main result tree (`context-menu`, [frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx)) — three decision items at its top. The Execute dialog's row menu ([frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx)) carries the same three.
- **Trigger:** Right-click a file row (single, or part of a multi-selection) and pick **Keep this file**, **Delete — move to Recycle Bin**, or **Skip now — drop from this review**.
- **Behaviour:** The items act on the target set: the whole multi-selection when the right-clicked row belongs to it, otherwise just that row (which then becomes the selection). **Keep** / **Delete** write `user_decision` `''` / `delete` for every target in one `PATCH /api/decision`, applied optimistically to the rows in place (no manifest reload) and reverted on any failure. If any target is locked the server answers 409 `locked_paths` and the lock-confirm dialog opens with the deferred-decision wording — **Cancel**, **Unlocked only** (re-sends the decision for the unlocked targets only) or **Unlock & Apply** (writes every target and unlocks the locked ones in the same write) — see [Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog). **Skip now** does not stage a decision: it finalizes the targets to `outcome='ignored'` at once via `POST /api/remove`, with no confirm ([#694](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/694)); the staged, reversible Skip lives on the row's own decision control and in the Set Action dialog — see [Bulk regex — remove from list (deferred decision)](#bulk-regex--remove-from-list-deferred-decision).
- **Conditions / variants:** The same menu also offers **Set Action by Field…** (opens the Set Action dialog pre-filled from the right-clicked column and seeded from that row, #735 / #893), **Execute Action (only selected)…**, Lock / Unlock, Open folder, and **Apply best-copy decisions to this group** ([#744](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/744)). The group-header menu has no Keep / Delete. In the Execute dialog's row menu the verbs act on the right-clicked row only, and its Skip finalizes that row behind a confirm ("Skip this file?").
- **Related:** Foundation in [PR #19](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/19); [#694](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/694); [#733](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/733) (decision writes lock-gated); [#735](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/735); selection behaviour under [Web — main result tree multi-selection](#web--main-result-tree-multi-selection); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py) (right-click Delete / Keep, By-Field and Execute-selected present, group-row reduced menu), [`qa/web/scenarios/s20_multi_remove_from_list.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s20_multi_remove_from_list.py) (multi-selection fan-out + target resolution) and [`qa/web/scenarios/s53_execute_dialog_lock_decision.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s53_execute_dialog_lock_decision.py) (Execute-dialog row menu Delete, and a locked row's 409 → lock-confirm → Cancel leaving the row byte-for-byte unchanged); unit [`frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.test.tsx`](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.test.tsx) and [`tests/test_web_review_routes.py`](../tests/test_web_review_routes.py) (`PATCH /api/decision`: 409 without flags, `force_locked` applies and unlocks, `skip_locked` writes the unlocked rows only).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Empty area context menu

- **Entry point:** None as a feature. The app's own context menu (`context-menu`, [frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx)) opens only from a right-click on a **file row** ([frontend/src/components/result/FileRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/FileRow.tsx)) or a **group header** ([frontend/src/components/result/GroupRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/GroupRow.tsx)); the tree's scroll container ([frontend/src/components/ResultTree.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ResultTree.tsx)), its column header and the menu bar carry no context-menu handler.
- **Trigger:** The user right-clicks a non-row surface — the empty area below the last row, or the empty gap of the menu bar.
- **Behaviour:** No app menu opens. The app mounts the menu only while its menu state is open, and only the file-row handler (the full, selection-aware file-row menu) and the group-header handler (the reduced group menu — Set Action by Field…, Skip now, Apply best-copy; #735 / #744) set that state. Nothing on the empty surface calls `preventDefault`, so the browser's own context menu may appear there instead. If an app menu is already open, the right-click lands outside it and dismisses it.
- **Conditions / variants:** Independent of the selection. A row right-click needs no prior left-click — the row under the pointer drives the menu, and a row outside the current selection becomes the new single selection.
- **Related:** QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s25_empty_area_context_menu.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s25_empty_area_context_menu.py) (branches A / B: no `context-menu` after right-clicking below the last row or in the menu-bar gap; C / D: a row right-click with and without a prior left-click does open it); menu contents under [Web — main result tree multi-selection](#web--main-result-tree-multi-selection).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client; the previous text described a global tree menu that the app never had)

### Empty-state action buttons

- **Entry point:** The main area's empty state (`main-empty-state`, rendered by [frontend/src/App.tsx](../frontend/src/App.tsx) in place of the result tree) — the primary **Scan…** (`main-empty-scan`) and the secondary **Open Manifest…** (`main-empty-open`).
- **Trigger:** No manifest is loaded and none is loading — every fresh page load until a scan finishes or a manifest is opened.
- **Behaviour:** **Scan…** opens the Scan dialog (the same handler as **File → Scan Sources…**). **Open Manifest…** opens the filesystem picker in file mode (the same handler as **File → Open Manifest…**); confirming a file loads it. The buttons sit under a heading ("Find duplicate & similar photos") and one sentence on what the app does; only **Scan…** is filled (the single-primary rule, layout slice E). No "nothing is deleted" safety line and no recent-sources list are drawn — both owner decisions. The labels come from the `web.empty_state.*` keys, translated in zh_TW (`掃描…` / `開啟清單…`). The Scan label differs from its menu item (Scan… vs File → Scan Sources…); Open Manifest… reads the same as File → Open Manifest….
- **Conditions / variants:** The whole empty state is replaced by the result tree as soon as a manifest path is set; while a manifest is loading, the tree area shows "Loading manifest…" instead.
- **Related:** [PR #197](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/197) (closes [#137](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/137)); layout slice E of [#878](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/878) — computed sizes and colours under [Web — Daylight theme and results-tree framing](#web--daylight-theme-and-results-tree-framing); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s41_empty_state_action_buttons.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s41_empty_state_action_buttons.py) (both buttons present; Scan opens `scan-dialog`, Open Manifest opens `fs-browser`) and [`qa/web/scenarios/s78_empty_state.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s78_empty_state.py) (the empty state at size, in both locales); unit [`frontend/src/App.test.tsx`](../frontend/src/App.test.tsx) (composition, zh_TW, both handlers, the two deliberate absences).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Execute Action — base flow

- **Entry point:** Menu bar **Action → Execute Action…** (`menu-action-execute`, [frontend/src/components/MenuBar.tsx](../frontend/src/components/MenuBar.tsx)) and the toolbar **Execute** button (`main-execute-button`, [frontend/src/components/Toolbar.tsx](../frontend/src/components/Toolbar.tsx)) — both disabled until a manifest is loaded — plus the toolbar's danger button **"⊗ Delete N files…"** (`main-delete-cta`, disabled while N is 0). All three call `store.openExecuteDialog()` ([frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts](../frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts)) with no scope. The dialog is [frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx), mounted once in [frontend/src/App.tsx](../frontend/src/App.tsx). The selection-scoped variant is [Web — Execute (only selected)](#web--execute-only-selected).
- **Trigger:** User picks any of the three entry points.
- **Behaviour:** Opens the **"Execute Actions — Review"** modal (`execute-dialog`). Its tree (`execute-tree`, [frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteTree.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteTree.tsx)) lists every row with a pending **Delete** or **Skip** decision (`user_decision` `delete` / `ignore`) under a `Group N` header row. Keep rows (`""`) are not listed, and a group with no decided row is left out. Each row (`execute-row-{group}-{basename}`) shows a 40 px thumbnail, the basename, the folder and a Delete / Skip badge. Above the tree sit the **Show:** type filter and a count of decided rows. A preview pane sits to its right. The footer holds **Cancel**, **Execute selected** and **Execute**.
  - **Execute** sends `POST /api/execute` ([app/web/routes/execute.py](../app/web/routes/execute.py)) with `recycle: true`; with the filter on "All decided" and no scope it sends `scope_paths: null`. The route runs `execute_decisions` ([core/app_service/execute_service.py](../core/app_service/execute_service.py)) off the event loop. The service re-reads decisions and lock state from SQLite, never trusting the client's copy, and considers only in-review rows (`outcome=''`). It then:
    - moves each **Delete** file to the Recycle Bin (`DeleteService.delete_to_recycle`, send2trash) and writes `outcome='deleted'` for that file straight after it succeeds. Because the write is per file, a crash mid-batch still leaves the DB consistent (D7).
    - finalizes every **Skip** row as `outcome='ignored'`. Those files stay on disk.
    - refuses any Delete path outside the scan's allowed roots and reports it as failed with reason `outside_allowed_roots`.
    - writes an audit CSV `delete_<timestamp>.csv` under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\PhotoManager\delete_logs`. The web path writes `0` in its GroupNumber column.
    - returns the reloaded groups.

    Locked Delete rows stop the run first with a 409 — see [Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog).
  - The store replaces `manifest.groups` with the response and clears the main-tree selection. It then runs the [singleton-prune offer](#singleton-prune-offer-after-destructive-ops-426). **The dialog stays open after Execute.** Finalized rows drop out of the review, so both trees now show only what is still pending.
  - **Per-file outcome ([#742](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/742)).** `ExecuteResultSummary` ([frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteResultSummary.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteResultSummary.tsx)) shows two sections inside the dialog:
    - **"Files Not Found"** (`execute-result-missing`, caution style) lists `executeResult.missing`: files already gone from disk. These are skipped and not marked deleted.
    - **"Files Failed to Delete"** (`execute-result-failed`, danger style) lists `executeResult.failed`, each path followed by its reason. `DeleteService.delete_to_recycle` ([infrastructure/delete_service.py](../infrastructure/delete_service.py)) decodes `OSError.winerror` through [infrastructure/winerror_reasons.py](../infrastructure/winerror_reasons.py), which holds the only reason table ([#757](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/757)). Examples are "file is in use by another process" (0x80270027), "access denied (source)" and "path too long for Recycle Bin". An unmapped error keeps its raw message.

    Missing and failed rows keep their Delete decision and stay in the tree, so a later Execute retries them. Nothing renders when both lists are empty. Errors that stop the whole operation show on a separate red alert line above the summary. Examples are a second Execute while one is running (409 `execute_already_running`) and a 422. The retired desktop client showed the two lists as blocking warning boxes.
  - **Cancel** or Escape closes the dialog without executing. Decisions are untouched because each one is already saved when it is set (see [Web — manifest decision persistence](#web--manifest-decision-persistence)).
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - **Execute** is disabled when no decided row is visible under the current filter. It is also disabled while a run is in flight: the label then reads "Executing…", Cancel is disabled and Escape is ignored. A click outside the dialog never closes it ([#721](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/721), see the lock-confirm entry).
  - Before the request, the client may show a confirm — see [Execute Action — complete-group delete confirm](#execute-action--complete-group-delete-confirm). The type filter and the in-dialog selection narrow what gets executed — see [filter by action type](#execute-action--filter-by-action-type-502) and [Execute selected](#execute-action--partial-execution-via-execute-selected).
  - **Row menu.** Right-clicking a row opens the dialog's own menu ([frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx)):
    - **Delete** / **Keep** set a decision via `PATCH /api/decision`.
    - **Skip** finalizes the row at once via `POST /api/remove`, after a "Skip this file?" confirm (`execute-remove-confirm`).
    - **Lock** / **Unlock**.
    - **Set Action by Field…** opens the same Set Action dialog the toolbar opens, with no row seed ([#893](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/893)). Its match runs over the whole manifest, not only the Execute dialog's groups. The retired desktop client limited its in-dialog "Select by Field/Regex…" to the groups shown ([#443](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/443)).
  - **One source of truth ([#444](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/444)).** The Execute tree and the main result tree both render from the store's `manifest.groups`. A decision or lock changed inside the dialog therefore shows in the main tree at once, whether or not the dialog is then executed.
  - The last run's per-file summary stays in the store until the next Execute starts. Closing and reopening the dialog does not clear it.
  - Moving, resizing and scrolling: see [Execute Action — dialog geometry persistence](#execute-action--dialog-geometry-persistence).
- **Related:** [#742](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/742) (Not Found / Failed split with decoded reasons), [#757](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/757) (single reason table), [#673](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/673) (toolbar Execute gated on a loaded manifest). Layer-1: `tests/test_winerror_reasons.py`, `tests/test_delete_service.py`, `tests/test_web_execute_service.py`, `tests/test_web_execute_routes.py`, `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.test.tsx`. QA scenarios:
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s13_execute_action.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s13_execute_action.py): the destructive round-trip, plus the mixed-manifest and missing-file phases.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s30_execute_dialog_regex_right_click.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s30_execute_dialog_regex_right_click.py): row menu → Set Action by Field.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s54_execute_dialog_remove_from_list.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s54_execute_dialog_remove_from_list.py): row menu → Skip.
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-07-04 (web parity #742)

### Execute Action — complete-group delete confirm

- **Entry point:** `DeleteConfirmDialog` ([frontend/src/components/dialogs/DeleteConfirmDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/dialogs/DeleteConfirmDialog.tsx), `execute-all-delete-confirm`). The Execute dialog's **Execute** and **Execute selected** handlers open it when `completeDeleteGroupIds(groups, scopePaths)` ([frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx)) returns at least one group.
- **Trigger:** User clicks **Execute** or **Execute selected** while at least one group would have every in-scope member deleted. The check runs in the browser before any request is sent.
- **Behaviour:** A blocking modal titled **"Entire group(s) will be deleted"** reads: "Group(s) N, M will have EVERY file deleted (K files will be deleted). Files will be sent to the Recycle Bin. Continue?" K counts every Delete row in the pending call's scope, not only the complete groups.
  - **Yes, delete all** (`execute-all-delete-confirm-yes`) sends the queued `POST /api/execute` with the same options.
  - **Cancel** (`execute-all-delete-confirm-no`) or Escape sends nothing. The Execute dialog stays open with its decisions intact.
  - **The auditable body ([#917](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/917), design REPLY Q6).** The dialog shows *what* is about to be deleted, not only how many.
    - **Layout.** The modal is at least **60vh** tall. The row list scrolls inside it, while the totals line and the confirm button stay pinned outside the scroll area, so the number never scrolls out of view.
    - **Folder buckets.** The Delete rows are **grouped by folder**. Each folder header (`execute-delete-confirm-folder`) shows the folder path plus its own count and size subtotal. This is the cue Q6 wants for "why is anything from *that* folder in here".
    - **One reason line per file** (`execute-delete-confirm-reason`, 12px muted). It spells out, in sentence form, the relationship the Similarity badge encodes (the badge itself reads 100% / N% / ★ N% / ~dup / — / Ref): "Exact duplicate of `<ref>`"／「與 `<ref>` 完全重複」, "N% match — same group"／「N% 相似 — 同一群組」, "Linked match, N%*"／「間接比對，N%*」. There is also a phrase each for `~dup`, "—", and a Ref row that is itself marked delete.
    - **Totals line.** A pinned line (`execute-delete-confirm-totals`) reads "N files · X MB", computed by the same rule as `lib/deleteTotals` — the same pair the status bar carries ([#906](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/906)/[#916](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/916)).
    - **Safety sentence.** **"Files are moved to the Recycle Bin and can be restored."**／**「檔案將移至資源回收筒，可還原。」** is always present.
    - **The list follows the execute SCOPE, not the group.** It is built from the same `scope_paths` the pending execute call will send, so its total matches what the server deletes. The footer's **Execute selected** lists exactly the rows selected in the Execute tree. A dialog opened with **Execute (only selected)** is scoped to whole groups, so its **Execute** lists every Delete row of those groups. Unscoped Execute lists every Delete row, as before. A row's reason line still names its group's Ref even when that Ref is itself out of scope — the keeper a file duplicates does not stop existing because it was not selected.
    - Bucketing and the reason wording live in [`frontend/src/lib/deleteConfirmRows.ts`](../frontend/src/lib/deleteConfirmRows.ts).
    - The whole body is additive. A caller that passes no groups (the Set Action dialog's bulk-decide gate) gets the unchanged compact dialog, and every existing testid, handler and gate condition is untouched.
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - **Which groups count ([#733](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/733)).** One qualifying group is enough to block.
    - **Unscoped Execute**, and a group-scoped dialog under the "All decided" filter, check each group as a whole: a group qualifies only when every member is Delete, undecided rows included.
    - **Other filters** check the visible decided rows instead (the #502 analog). Under "Delete only" any group with a visible Delete row qualifies; under "Skip only" none does.
    - **Execute selected** checks the selected rows only. A group qualifies when its selected rows are all Delete, even if other rows of that group are outside the selection. The list then shows exactly the selected rows.
  - **History.** Before #733 the web gate fired only when the whole scope was 100% Delete. A mixed manifest — one complete-delete group plus one group with keeps — then executed with **no blocking confirm** at all.
  - **Order.** This confirm comes before the request. A locked Delete row therefore raises its lock prompt only after **Yes** (the 409, see [Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog)). The retired desktop client asked about locks first; the outcome is the same.
  - The passive, non-blocking version of this warning is [the complete-group banner](#execute-action--complete-group-warning-banner-with-jump-to).
- **Related:** [#733](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/733) (per-group gate), [#917](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/917) (auditable body); desktop precursors [PR #30](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/30) (single confirm) and [PR #219](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/219) (scope-narrowed check). Layer-1: `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.test.tsx` (`completeDeleteGroupIds`), `frontend/src/components/dialogs/DeleteConfirmDialog.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/lib/deleteConfirmRows.test.ts`. Layer-3: [`qa/web/scenarios/s13_execute_action.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s13_execute_action.py) (mixed-manifest phase, en + zh_TW).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-07-03 (web parity #733); 2026-09-19 (#917 confirm body)

### Execute Action — complete-group warning banner with jump-to

- **Entry point:** `AllDeleteBanner` ([frontend/src/components/execute/AllDeleteBanner.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/AllDeleteBanner.tsx), `execute-all-delete-banner`) above the Execute dialog's tree. `findAllDeleteGroupIds` in [frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx) supplies the groups.
- **Trigger:** Shown whenever at least one group in the dialog's scope is all-Delete under the active filter. It is recomputed on every decision, filter or scope change.
- **Behaviour:** A caution strip (▲ glyph plus **"Warning:"**) reads "Group(s) N, M will have ALL files deleted. Review decisions below before clicking Execute."
  - **One translated template.** The sentence is `web.execute_dialog.warning_complete_groups` (copy audit E2), with a singular or plural "Group"/"Groups". Each group number is a clickable button (`execute-all-delete-jump-{N}`) inserted at the template's `{groups}` marker. A locale can therefore reorder the sentence without losing the buttons (zh_TW currently keeps the group list in the same leading slot as en), and a translation that drops the marker still renders the full sentence with the buttons at the end.
  - **Jump.** Clicking a number scrolls the virtualized tree so that group's header row is at the top. It does not select a row; the retired desktop client also selected the group's row.
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - Which groups count depends on the filter ([#676](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/676)):
    - "All decided": a group counts only when every member is Delete, undecided rows included.
    - "Delete only": any group with a visible Delete row counts.
    - "Skip only": no group counts.

    So the buttons only point at groups the filtered tree actually shows.
  - In a dialog opened with [Execute (only selected)](#execute-action--scope-to-selected-groups-only-selected-entry-410-430-429), only the scoped groups count.
  - A number with no header row in the current tree does nothing, silently.
  - The banner only informs. The blocking gate is [the complete-group delete confirm](#execute-action--complete-group-delete-confirm). The separate "pending deletes hidden by the filter" banner is described under [filter by action type](#execute-action--filter-by-action-type-502).
- **Related:** [PR #181](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/181) (closes [#166](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/166)); [#676](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/676) (filter-narrowed). Layer-1: `frontend/src/components/execute/banners.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.test.tsx`. QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s33_execute_dialog_jump_to_all_delete.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s33_execute_dialog_jump_to_all_delete.py) clicks the real jump button (the retired desktop run could not click its label link on hosted CI).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Execute Action — dialog geometry persistence

- **Entry point:** The Execute dialog's header (`execute-title-bar`) doubles as a drag bar, and a bottom-right grip (`execute-resize-handle`) resizes it. Both come from `useOverlayGeometry("execute", …)` in [frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx) — the shared mechanism documented in [Web — overlay window geometry (movable / resizable / persisted)](#web--overlay-window-geometry-movable--resizable--persisted).
- **Trigger:** User drags the header or the grip. The saved geometry is applied every time the dialog opens, including after a page reload.
- **Behaviour:**
  - Geometry lives under its own localStorage key, `pm.overlay-geometry.execute.v1`.
  - A **move** stores position only; the dialog keeps sizing itself to its content.
  - Only a **resize** pins width and height. After that the body (`execute-body`) scrolls while the header and the Cancel / Execute selected / Execute footer stay in view.
  - Clamping to the viewport, the 320×280 px minimum and the fallback on unreadable storage follow the shared rules in the linked entry.
  - With nothing stored, the dialog opens at its default centered `max-w-4xl` layout.
- **Conditions / variants:** There is no divider between the tree and the preview pane — the preview has a fixed width — so there is no splitter position to save. The retired desktop client saved one. The same mechanism also serves the Set Action dialog and the full-resolution viewer; the Scan dialog stays centered and cannot be moved.
- **Related:** [#739](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/739) (divergence row D6); QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s48_dialog_geometry_persist.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s48_dialog_geometry_persist.py) (Execute and Set Action dialogs: drag, resize, reopen, reload, own key); unit tests [`frontend/src/lib/overlayGeometry.test.ts`](../frontend/src/lib/overlayGeometry.test.ts) and [`frontend/src/hooks/useOverlayGeometry.test.tsx`](../frontend/src/hooks/useOverlayGeometry.test.tsx).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog

- **Entry point:** `LockConfirmDialog` ([frontend/src/components/dialogs/LockConfirmDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/dialogs/LockConfirmDialog.tsx), `lock-confirm-dialog`), mounted once in [frontend/src/App.tsx](../frontend/src/App.tsx) and driven by `store.execute.lockConflict` (`{paths, op, originalPaths}`). It opens when a mutating request answers **409 `{"code": "locked_paths", "locked_paths": [...]}`**:

  | Request | `op` |
  |---|---|
  | `POST /api/execute` | `execute` |
  | `POST /api/remove` | `remove` |
  | `PATCH /api/decision` | `decision` ([#733](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/733)) |
  | `POST /api/action/apply-best-copy` | `apply-best-copy` ([#744](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/744)) |

  It also opens with no request at all, for locked singletons before a prune (`prune`, [#686](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/686)).
- **Trigger:** The server re-reads `is_locked` from SQLite on each such call and never trusts the client. In user terms, any of:
  - **Execute** / **Execute selected** with a locked Delete row in scope.
  - A finalizing **Skip** that includes a locked row — from the Execute dialog's row menu, the main-tree context menu, or **List → Skip Selected now**.
  - A decision change on a locked row. Right-click Keep/Delete in either tree and the `d`/`k` shortcuts go through `store.setDecisions`. The row's own Keep/Delete/Skip control and the preview pane's decision buttons are disabled on a locked row, so they never reach the gate.
  - **Apply best-copy decisions to this group** (group context menu) on a group with locked rows.
  - A [singleton-prune offer](#singleton-prune-offer-after-destructive-ops-426) that includes locked singletons.
- **Behaviour:** The dialog lists the locked paths and offers three buttons:
  - **Unlock & Apply** (`lock-confirm-btn-unlock-apply`) re-runs the same operation over the **same original scope** with `force_locked`. This unlocks the locked rows in the same write: Execute then deletes them, and a decision write lands with the row unlocked. Re-using the original scope is deliberate — re-running unscoped after an "Execute selected" would execute the whole manifest.
  - **Unlocked only** (`lock-confirm-btn-unlocked-only`) re-runs over the original scope minus the locked paths (`apply-best-copy` re-runs with `skip_locked`). Locked rows keep their lock and decision.
  - **Cancel** (`lock-confirm-btn-cancel`) clears the conflict; nothing is written.
  - **The wording states the consequence** (the desktop's [#417](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/417) principle). Title and body depend on `op` (`web.lock_confirm.*`):
    - `execute` — **"Locked files in delete scope"**: "N locked file(s) is/are about to be deleted (sent to the Recycle Bin). Unlock and proceed, or leave the locked files out." An earlier "permanently DELETED" claim was corrected; s34 asserts "permanent" is absent.
    - `remove` — **"Locked files in skip scope"**: "…about to be skipped (left on disk, dropped from this review)…"
    - `decision` — **"Locked rows affected"**: "Setting this decision would change M row(s); N is/are locked. **Nothing is deleted yet — this only queues the decision.** Unlock and set all, set only the unlocked rows, or cancel."
    - `apply-best-copy` — **"Locked rows in this group"**: "…Nothing is deleted yet — this only queues keep/delete decisions…"
    - `prune` — **"Locked singleton groups"**: "N locked singleton group(s) would be pruned. Unlock and prune it/them, or keep it/them in the list."

    **Unlock & Apply** is styled as destructive except for `decision` and `apply-best-copy`, which only queue decisions.
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - **Lock / Unlock never prompt** — `PATCH /api/lock` is not gated.
  - **At Execute, only locked Delete rows trip the gate.** A locked row already marked Skip is finalized without a prompt. Changing a locked row's decision is itself gated, so this only happens when the Skip was set before the row was locked.
  - **Not every path uses this dialog.**
    - The toolbar's bulk Keep/Delete/Skip verbs leave locked rows out in the browser, and the group header's Keep-best button sends `skip_locked` so the server leaves them alone. Both report "N locked file(s) unchanged" in their undo toast.
    - The Set Action by Field dialog has its own inline three-button prompt (`action-lock-confirm-dialog`). Its **Apply to Unlocked Only** is disabled when the 409's `matched_total` leaves no unlocked row. Cancelling that prompt keeps the Set Action dialog open with the user's field, regex and action ([#764](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/764), the twin of #721): `ActionDialog.onInteractOutside` always calls `preventDefault`, and Escape is guarded only while the dialog is applying.
  - **Unlocked only is never disabled here.** When every targeted row is locked it re-runs over whatever is left (for `decision`, nothing). The retired desktop client disabled it in that case.
  - **`prune`:** Cancel does not abort the prune. Cancel and **Unlocked only** both keep the locked singletons and go on to the unlocked ones; **Unlock & Apply** unlocks and prunes them.
  - **Order at Execute:** the [complete-group delete confirm](#execute-action--complete-group-delete-confirm) runs in the browser first; the 409 and this dialog come after **Yes**. The retired desktop client asked about locks first; the outcome is the same.
  - **Cancel keeps the Execute dialog open ([#721](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/721)).** The user adjusts and retries. The lock prompt is a separate Radix modal mounted beside the Execute dialog, not inside it. When it closes on Cancel it fires two "outside" events on the Execute dialog: a `focusin` as focus returns, then, once closed, a `pointerdown` that nothing blocks any more. `ExecuteDialog.onInteractOutside` therefore calls `preventDefault` **every time**, so a stray backdrop click cannot dismiss a destructive review dialog either. `onEscapeKeyDown` blocks only while `executeRunning`, so an idle dialog still closes on Escape; Radix sends Escape to the topmost layer only. This is proven live by s34 — jsdom cannot reproduce the focus-return / `pointerdown` sequence.
  - **History (#733).** Before #733 the web wrote decisions to locked rows **silently**. The only guard was the single-row dropdown's client-side `disabled`, which the context menus and shortcuts bypassed. The same change made bulk-decide's `force_locked` also **unlock** the forced rows (`batch_update_decisions_and_lock`). Before that, the decision landed but the row stayed locked, so the next Execute tripped the gate again.
- **Related:** [PR #183](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/183) (closes [#182](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/182)); [#417](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/417), [#733](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/733), [#721](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/721), [#764](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/764), [#674](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/674). Layer-1: `tests/test_review_service.py`, `tests/test_web_review_routes.py`, `tests/test_web_execute_routes.py`, `frontend/src/components/dialogs/LockConfirmDialog.test.tsx`. QA scenarios:
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s34_lock_confirm_at_execute.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s34_lock_confirm_at_execute.py): `execute`, Cancel — the Execute dialog stays open; asserts the delete wording.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s36_lock_confirm_destructive_execute.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s36_lock_confirm_destructive_execute.py): `execute`, Unlock & Apply.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s53_execute_dialog_lock_decision.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s53_execute_dialog_lock_decision.py): `decision`, via the Execute dialog's row menu.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s32_lock_confirm_bulk_regex.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s32_lock_confirm_bulk_regex.py): the Set Action inline prompt — #764 Cancel plus Apply to Unlocked Only.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s61_actioned_singleton_prune.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s61_actioned_singleton_prune.py) / [`qa/web/scenarios/s67_locked_singleton_prune_always.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s67_locked_singleton_prune_always.py): `prune`.
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-07-03 (web parity #733); 2026-07-05 (web cancel-parity #721 + Set Action twin #764)

### Execute Action — preview pane

- **Entry point:** The `execute-preview-pane` panel to the right of the Execute dialog's tree ([frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx)).
- **Trigger:** Always shown while the dialog is open. Clicking a row makes it the preview target.
- **Behaviour:** A fixed-width (224 px) panel shows the **thumbnail** of the last-clicked row as a still image (`execute-preview-image`). The image comes from the row's `/api/image?path=…&size=512` URL and is at most 192 px tall. Before any click the panel reads "Select a row to preview". It has no metadata, zoom or video playback; it is not the main window's preview pane. The preview follows the last-clicked row even with several rows selected: a Ctrl+click that removes a row from the selection still previews it.
- **Conditions / variants:** The pane cannot be resized; there is no divider (see [dialog geometry persistence](#execute-action--dialog-geometry-persistence)). If the previewed row leaves the dialog's groups — for example because it was executed — the placeholder returns. The retired desktop client embedded its full preview pane here.
- **Related:** [#165](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/165) ([PR #260](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/260)) introduced the pane. QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s51_execute_dialog_preview.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s51_execute_dialog_preview.py) clicks a row, asserts the image is visible, closes with Escape and checks decisions are unchanged. Layer-1: `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.test.tsx` ("preview pane is rendered").
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Execute Action — partial execution via "Execute selected"

- **Entry point:** The **Execute selected** button (`execute-btn-execute-selected`) in the Execute dialog's footer, between Cancel and Execute (`handleExecuteSelected` in [frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.tsx)).
- **Trigger:** User selects one or more rows in the dialog's tree and clicks the button.
  - **Selection.** The tree allows several rows at once ([#697](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/697), [frontend/src/lib/multiSelect.ts](../frontend/src/lib/multiSelect.ts)). A plain click replaces the selection, Ctrl/Cmd+click adds or removes a row, and Shift+click selects the range from the last click over the visible row order. Selected rows carry `aria-selected="true"`.
  - This selection belongs to the dialog and is separate from the main tree's.
- **Behaviour:** Runs the standard pipeline narrowed to the selected paths:
  1. The [complete-group confirm](#execute-action--complete-group-delete-confirm) is checked over the selection.
  2. `POST /api/execute` is sent with `scope_paths` set to the selection.
  3. The lock gate, per-file Recycle Bin delete, Skip finalize, audit CSV and prune offer then run as in the [base flow](#execute-action--base-flow).

  The server acts only on decided, in-review rows in that list, so every row outside it keeps its decision. Executed rows leave both trees. The dialog stays open so the user can keep reviewing — on the web, full Execute leaves it open as well.
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - Disabled when nothing is selected, or while a run is in flight.
  - **The selection is not intersected with the type filter.** A row selected under one filter stays selected — and in the Execute-selected scope — after a switch to a filter that hides it. The selection is also not cleared after a run; paths that were executed simply match nothing on the next click.
  - It combines with the dialog's own scope. [Execute (only selected)](#execute-action--scope-to-selected-groups-only-selected-entry-410-430-429) first limits the dialog to the selected rows' groups, and Execute selected narrows further inside them. The in-dialog selection is not intersected with those groups, though, and it survives closing the dialog. A row selected during an earlier, unscoped open can therefore still be in the list **Execute selected** sends, even when its group is not on screen.
  - Partial execution is the main source of **actioned** singletons — one row left in a group with a decision that was not executed. See [Singleton-prune offer after destructive ops](#singleton-prune-offer-after-destructive-ops-426).
- **Related:** [#697](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/697) (in-dialog multi-select); [#410](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/410) (a second button rather than a relabelled Execute). QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s64_execute_selected_partial.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s64_execute_selected_partial.py): a plain click plus a Ctrl+click selects two rows; Execute selected; asserts those two are gone and the three survivors keep Delete with the dialog still open; then a full Execute clears the rest. Layer-1: `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/lib/multiSelect.test.ts`.
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Execute Action — filter by action type (#502)

- **Entry point:** The **Show:** select (`execute-type-filter`, [frontend/src/components/execute/TypeFilter.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/TypeFilter.tsx)) in the Execute dialog's toolbar row. It has three options — **All decided** (default), **Delete only** and **Skip only** — whose wire values are `all` / `delete` / `ignore`. The labels are translated and use the Keep / Delete / Skip wording (copy audit E1).
- **Trigger:** User changes the selection.
- **Behaviour:** The filter narrows what is shown and what is executed; it never changes a decision.
  - **Display.** The tree shows only decided rows of the chosen type, leaving out groups with none. The decided-row count updates and both banners are recomputed.
  - **"Visible = committed" ([#502](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/502), web [#676](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/676)).** Under any filter other than "All decided", **Execute** sends `scope_paths` set to exactly the visible decided rows. Rows of the other type keep their decisions for a later run. Before #676 the web sent `scope_paths=null` and executed every decided row whatever the filter — a user filtered to "Delete only" still executed the hidden Skip rows, and vice versa.
  - **Hidden-destructive banner.** Under **Skip only**, `HiddenDestructiveBanner` ([frontend/src/components/execute/HiddenDestructiveBanner.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/HiddenDestructiveBanner.tsx), `execute-hidden-destructive-banner`) warns: "N pending delete row(s) hidden by the current filter — switch to All decided or Delete only to see them." So a Skip-only view never hides that deletions are staged. The text is `web.execute_dialog.warning_hidden_destructive`, with "row"/"rows" chosen by count (copy audit E3); it used to be hardcoded English with a flattened "row(s)".
  - **All-delete banner.** It is narrowed to the filter as well — see [the complete-group banner](#execute-action--complete-group-warning-banner-with-jump-to).
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - **No Keep or Lock option.** Keep (`""`) rows never appear in the Execute tree, and a lock is not a decision.
  - **With the lock gate:** a Skip-only Execute covers only Skip rows, so locked Delete rows outside it do not raise the [lock prompt](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog).
  - **With the complete-group confirm:** under a filter other than "All decided", the [confirm](#execute-action--complete-group-delete-confirm) checks the visible rows.
  - **With "Execute selected":** the selection **is** intersected with the filter — [Execute selected](#execute-action--partial-execution-via-execute-selected) sends only the selected rows the filter still shows, and is disabled when none of them is visible (#940, below).
  - The filter lives in the dialog and is not reset when the dialog closes. It stays set across close and reopen and returns to "All decided" only on a page reload. The retired desktop client reset it on every reopen.
- **Web parity — Execute selected (#940):** the highlight and the filter now intersect on the web too. `handleExecuteSelected` sends only the highlighted rows the active filter still shows (`visibleSelectedPaths` = the highlight ∩ `decidedPaths`), the complete-delete confirm judges that same narrowed set, and the button is disabled when no highlighted row is visible. Previously the raw highlight was sent: a delete row highlighted under "All decided" and then hidden by "Skip only" was executed alongside the visible skip row, with no confirm, right under the banner that said it was out of scope. Switching back to a filter that shows the row makes it part of the scope again, because it is still highlighted. The set is also clamped to the dialog's in-scope groups. The dialog stays mounted, so a highlight survives Cancel, and reopening via "Execute (only selected)" on other groups could otherwise send a row that is not in the tree. Layer-1 coverage: `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.test.tsx`. Layer-3: s60 asserts that the button is disabled when its only highlighted row is hidden.
- **Related:** [#502](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/502), [#676](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/676). Composes with [Execute selected](#execute-action--partial-execution-via-execute-selected), [scope to selected groups](#execute-action--scope-to-selected-groups-only-selected-entry-410-430-429), the [lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog) and the [complete-group banner](#execute-action--complete-group-warning-banner-with-jump-to). Layer-1: `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteDialog.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/execute/banners.test.tsx`. Layer-3: [`qa/web/scenarios/s60_execute_filter_by_action_type.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s60_execute_filter_by_action_type.py).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-09-30 (#940 — Execute selected intersects the highlight with the visible rows); 2026-06-23 (web parity #676 — ExecuteDialog filter-scoped commit + HiddenDestructiveBanner; web s60 scenario added + live-verified)

### Execute Action — scope to selected groups (only-selected entry, #410, #430, #429)

- **Entry point:** Menu bar **Action → Execute Action (only selected)…** (`menu-action-execute-selected`), and the item with the same label in a file row's context menu (`ctx-execute-selected`, [#735](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/735), the web counterpart of [#429](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/429)). Full description: [Web — Execute (only selected)](#web--execute-only-selected).
- **Trigger:** At least one row selected in the main result tree, with a manifest loaded. The menu-bar item is disabled otherwise.
- **Behaviour:** Opens the Execute dialog scoped by **group membership** ([#430](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/430)). Every selected row pulls in its whole group, recorded as `store.execute.scopeGroupNumbers`. Everything the dialog derives from groups is limited to those groups: the tree, both banners, the complete-group confirm and what Execute commits. The Execute label stays "Execute" ([#410](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/410)). The scope is cleared when the dialog closes.
- **Conditions / variants:** Groups arrive whole. Under "All decided", the [complete-group confirm](#execute-action--complete-group-delete-confirm) therefore fires only when every row of a group, undecided rows included, is Delete. To narrow further to individual rows inside the dialog, use [Execute selected](#execute-action--partial-execution-via-execute-selected).
- **Related:** [#410](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/410), [#430](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/430), [#429](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/429) / [#735](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/735). QA scenarios:
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s44_execute_selected_only.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s44_execute_selected_only.py): pulls in a whole group and confines the dialog to it, across two groups.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py): #735 context-menu phase.
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client)

### Exit dirty-flag prompt

- **Entry point:** None — removed with the desktop client in #646.
- **Trigger:** None on the web.
- **Behaviour:** The retired desktop client offered a three-button **Save & leave / Leave / Back** prompt when the window closed after decision changes. The web app has no unsaved state to protect: every decision is written to the manifest as it is made (see [Web — manifest decision persistence](#web--manifest-decision-persistence)), and a browser tab cannot show a custom close dialog. Its only close-time guard is the browser's own Leave/Stay prompt, armed only while a scan is running (see [Main window — close-during-scan confirm](#main-window--close-during-scan-confirm)).
- **Conditions / variants:** None.
- **Related:** [PR #158](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/158) (desktop). QA scenario `s28_exit_dirty_prompt` is `status: skip` in [`qa/web/scenario_map.yml`](../qa/web/scenario_map.yml) (desktop-only, #688). The scan-time close guard is covered by [`qa/web/scenarios/s63_late_cancel_and_main_window_guard.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s63_late_cancel_and_main_window_guard.py).

### Singleton-prune offer after destructive ops (#426)

- **Entry point:** `store.maybeOfferPrune` ([frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts](../frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts)). It runs at the end of every successful `executeDecisions` (Execute / Execute selected) and every finalizing `removeFromList`: **Skip** from the Execute dialog's row menu, the main-tree context menu, or **List → Skip Selected now**.
  - **Dialogs.** `PruneConfirmDialog` ([frontend/src/components/dialogs/PruneConfirmDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/dialogs/PruneConfirmDialog.tsx), `prune-confirm-dialog`) and, for locked singletons, the [lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog) with `op="prune"`.
  - **Setting.** **Settings → Prune singletons** (`settings-prune-select`, [frontend/src/components/SettingsDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/SettingsDialog.tsx)) offers **Ask each time / Always prune / Never prune**. It is stored as `ui.prune_singletons` in `settings.json` (`"ask"` in `settings.json.example`); any other value is read as "ask".
- **Trigger:** A destructive op leaves one or more groups with exactly one row still under review.
  - The review drops single-member groups, so the browser cannot see singletons itself. Instead `POST /api/prune/candidates` (`classify_singletons` in [core/app_service/execute_service.py](../core/app_service/execute_service.py)) sorts **every** current singleton — not only those the last op created — into three groups:
    - **locked** — locked, whatever its decision;
    - **actioned** — unlocked, with a pending Delete or Skip;
    - **plain** — unlocked, with no decision.

    Rows outside the allowed roots are left out.
  - Nothing happens when all three are empty, or when that call fails.
- **Behaviour:**
  - The setting is re-read from `GET /api/settings` every time. With **Never prune**, nothing happens.
  - **Locked singletons first ([#584](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/584) D6).** On both **Ask** and **Always**, locked singletons first raise the lock prompt:
    - **Unlock & Apply** unlocks them and adds them to the prune set, whatever the next dialog decides.
    - **Unlocked only** and **Cancel** both keep them and continue with the unlocked ones.
  - **Always prune** prunes the plain and actioned singletons, plus any just unlocked, in one call.
  - **Ask each time** opens **"Prune singleton groups?"**, listing the candidate paths, in one of three layouts:
    - *plain only* — "N singleton group(s) now have only one file remaining. Prune … from the list?"
    - *actioned only* — "N singleton group(s) with an un-executed delete / skip action remain. Prune them from the list?" Here **Prune** itself includes the actioned ones.
    - *mixed* — the plain count, plus "…and M more carry an un-executed delete / skip action", plus a checkbox **"Also prune M actioned singleton(s)"** (`prune-include-actioned`). The checkbox starts **unchecked** and is not remembered.
  - **Buttons.** **Keep all** (`prune-btn-keep`) and **Prune N** (`prune-btn-remove`). N is the plain count, or the actioned count when there are no plain singletons. With **Don't ask again** (`prune-remember`) ticked, Prune switches the setting to Always and Keep all switches it to Never. Escape or a click outside counts as Keep all.
  - **Applying the prune.** `POST /api/prune` receives the exact path list (the explicit mode of `prune_singletons`). It re-checks that each path is still an unlocked singleton inside the allowed roots, then finalizes it as `outcome='ignored'`; the file on disk is not touched. The returned groups replace the store's. A failed prune shows on the status bar's error line.
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - **Keep all** and **Never prune** leave the singleton in the manifest with `outcome=''`. It stays hidden, as a single-member group, and is offered again after the next destructive op.
  - A kept **actioned** singleton keeps its pending decision while hidden, and no Execute, scoped or unscoped, acts on it: Execute considers a decided row only while its group still has two or more rows under review (#941, below).
  - Partial execution via [Execute selected](#execute-action--partial-execution-via-execute-selected) is the most common source of actioned singletons.
- **Related:** [#426](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/426), [#686](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/686) (web port), [#584](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/584) (D6 locked-singleton gate), [#589](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/589), [#713](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/713) / [#702](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/702) (no second prune after the dialog closes itself); precedent for a batched confirm is [#417](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/417). Layer-1: `tests/test_web_execute_service.py` (classify + explicit prune), `tests/test_web_execute_routes.py`, `frontend/src/lib/prune.test.ts`, `frontend/src/components/dialogs/PruneConfirmDialog.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/store/useAppStore.test.ts`. QA scenarios:
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s61_actioned_singleton_prune.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s61_actioned_singleton_prune.py): **Ask** — seven variants, including all three lock-prompt answers.
  - [`qa/web/scenarios/s67_locked_singleton_prune_always.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s67_locked_singleton_prune_always.py): **Always** — the lock prompt still fires.
- **A kept singleton is never executed (#941):** Execute acts only on rows the review shows. `_load_decided_rows` ([core/app_service/execute_service.py](../core/app_service/execute_service.py)) considers a decided row only if its group still has ≥ 2 in-review members, the same survivor rule as the review's orphan-skip. An actioned singleton the user keeps (Keep all, Remove with the opt-in unchecked, or `"never"`) therefore stays exactly as the prune offer left it: outcome `''`, its pending decision intact, and a prune candidate at the next offer. Previously the server executed every in-review decided row. A plain Execute then deleted the hidden row, even though it had appeared in no tree, file count or #733 complete-delete confirm. The common producer was a partial "Execute selected" that left the second copy of an all-delete group alone. This matches Qt after a reload, where `load()` drops the singleton from `vm.groups` too. Layer-1 coverage: `tests/test_web_execute_service.py::TestExecuteDecisions::test_unscoped_execute_skips_actioned_singleton_hidden_from_review` (real SQLite + real files).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-09-30 (#941 — execute skips singletons the review hides); 2026-06-24 (#686 — web port)

### Keep-worthiness scoring

- **Entry point:** The **Score** column of the main result tree — the fourth column, after File Name / Similarity / Action ([frontend/src/lib/resultColumns.ts](../frontend/src/lib/resultColumns.ts)). [frontend/src/components/result/FileRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/FileRow.tsx) renders the score to two decimals (`—` when it is null) under a small "keep" label, over a mini bar. `GET /api/manifest` returns every group's rows score-descending, so with no column sort active the best copy is at the top of every group.
- **Trigger:** Automatic — every scanned file is scored at scan time and the score is stored in the manifest; no user action. `ManifestRepository.rescore(manifest_path, weights)` recomputes the scores from the cached raw signals without re-scanning (no file I/O); it is a repository method with no web UI or API entry point.
- **Behaviour:** Composite score in `[0.0, 1.0]` measuring how "keep-worthy" each file is, computed as a pure function of file attributes (no user-intent signals). Two-tier algorithm: tier 1 absolute penalties (format, `xmpMM:DerivedFrom`); tier 2 weighted composite of eight continuous signals (resolution, EXIF completeness, date provenance, filename, GPS, path, Live Photo, file size). Live Photo MOV passengers get `score = NULL` and are skipped by ranking. They do **not** inherit the paired still's decision: auto-select and Apply best-copy leave them untouched, so a still marked Delete leaves its MOV at Keep, and Execute does not delete the MOV unless it is decided separately. A video is a passenger when a same-stem, same-folder still sibling is a Live Photo image: HEIC/HEIF or, since #738, JPG/JPEG (iPhone's "Most Compatible" mode exports Live Photos as JPG+MOV). The HEIC-orphan completeness penalty stays HEIC/HEIF-only — a lone JPG is an ordinary photo, not a lost-pair. **Within-group order:** the review view prepends a score-descending sort ahead of any `sorting.defaults` in settings.json (those act as tiebreakers; listing `score` there explicitly replaces the implicit key). The Score column itself is not click-sortable; clicking **File Name** or **Size** re-sorts each group on the client, and because that sort is stable, rows that tie keep their score order (see [Main window — sort persistence within session](#main-window--sort-persistence-within-session)).
- **Conditions / variants:** Three surfaces turn the score into staged decisions, and all three share one top-N-by-score ranking rule ([#778](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/778), pinned by `tests/test_topn_keeper_parity.py`): the Set Action dialog's **Top N per group** condition on the Score field (see [Set Action dialog — numeric comparison panel](#set-action-dialog--numeric-comparison-panel)); **Apply best-copy decisions to this group**, reachable from the result-tree right-click menu and from the group header's **Keep best · delete rest** button, both calling `store.applyBestCopy` (see [Web — Context menu: Apply best-copy decisions to this group](#web--context-menu-apply-best-copy-decisions-to-this-group)); and auto-select after scan (see [Scan dialog — auto-select after scan](#scan-dialog--auto-select-after-scan)). The right-click item goes through the 409 lock-confirm dialog when the group has locked rows; the header button sends `skipLocked`, so locked rows keep their decision and their lock with no dialog. The right-click action had been removed from the retired desktop client in [PR #224](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/224) (closes [#210](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/210)) and came back web-only in #744.
- **Per-dimension signals on the manifest API ([#680](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/680)):** every `FileRow` returned by `GET /api/manifest` carries the raw scoring signals `gps_present`, `exif_tag_count` and `xmp_derived` alongside the composite `score`. Nullability mirrors the manifest columns: `exif_tag_count` may be `null` (the extended EXIF census pass did not run for that file — NOT the same as zero tags), while `gps_present` / `xmp_derived` are `NOT NULL` in SQLite and always arrive as booleans. They are produced by extraction rather than by scoring, so a row whose `score` is `null` (isolated row, Live Photo MOV passenger) still carries real values. Purely additive, and no UI surfaces them yet; the consumer is the web QA harness, which previously could only assert the aggregate score.
- **Related:** Cluster originated in [#187](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/187): [PR #199](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/199), [#200](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/200), [#202](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/202), [#203](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/203), [#204](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/204), [#205](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/205), [#206](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/206). Scorer in `scanner/scoring.py` (unit tests `tests/test_scoring.py`); within-group score-DESC default in `app/viewmodels/main_vm.py` (pinned by `tests/test_main_vm.py`), used by `core/app_service/review_service.py`; serialisation in `core/app_service/review_view.py` (`_build_file_row`), the DB read in `infrastructure/manifest_repository.py` (`_LOAD_ALL_SQL` / `_photo_record`), the client type in [`frontend/src/api/types.ts`](../frontend/src/api/types.ts) (`FileRow`). QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s42_scoring.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s42_scoring.py) (score range, score-DESC order within each group and, since #680, the per-dimension signals via `GET /api/manifest`), [`qa/web/scenarios/s56_action_dialog_apply_by_score.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s56_action_dialog_apply_by_score.py), [`qa/web/scenarios/s72_apply_best_copy.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s72_apply_best_copy.py), [`qa/web/scenarios/s75_keep_best_button.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s75_keep_best_button.py). Algorithm details in [README.md § Keep-worthiness scoring](../README.md#keep-worthiness-scoring-187).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#888 — rewritten against the shipped web client); 2026-07-03 (#738 — the Live Photo MOV-passenger rule now recognises JPG/JPEG stills, not just HEIC/HEIF); 2026-09-05 (#680 — per-dimension signals added to the web manifest API, verified live against `GET /api/manifest` and by the web s42 driver)
