### Web — Action dialog (Set Action by Field)

- **Entry point:** Toolbar `main-action-button` ("Set Action…", key `web.toolbar.set_action` — it used to borrow the dialog's own title key, whose value is the longer "Set Action by Field"; copy audit T1) — disabled when `manifest.path === null`; enabled once a scan result is loaded. `onClick` dispatches `store.openActionDialog()`. **Also reachable from the row right-click (#735):** the result-tree file-row context menu's `ctx-set-action-by-field` item (and the group-row reduced menu's item of the same testid) call `store.openActionDialog(initialField?)` — see [Web — main result tree multi-selection](#web--main-result-tree-multi-selection) for the menu itself.
- **Trigger:** User clicks the toolbar "Set Action…" button, picks menu bar **Action → "Set Action by Field…"** (the menu entry used to read "Set Action by Regex", disagreeing with this dialog's own title and with the right-click entry — regex is one of its three modes; copy audit M1), or right-clicks a file/group row and picks "Set Action by Field…". The `action-dialog` (`<dialog>` / Radix `DialogContent`) mounts when `store.action.actionDialogOpen === true`.
- **Behaviour:**
  - **Field selection** (`action-field-combo`) — `<select>` with 11 canonical English options (File Name, Score, Size (Bytes), Group Count, Similarity, Creation Date, Shot Date, …). Default value is "File Name".
  - **String-field mode** (field NOT in `NUMERIC_FIELDS`): shows `action-simple-row` (op combo + text input) and `action-regex-row` (direct regex). Simple-mode inputs write through to `action-regex-input` (the single source of truth): op "contains" + text "q9" produces the synthesised pattern `q9`; op "starts\_with" produces `^q9`; etc. The validation icon (`action-validation-icon`, span) shows ○ / ✓ / ✗ depending on whether the current pattern is empty / valid / invalid.
  - **Numeric-field mode** (field in `NUMERIC_FIELDS`: Size (Bytes), Group Count, Similarity, Score, Creation Date, Shot Date): `action-numeric-row` replaces the string rows. Two sub-modes — **Threshold** (`action-numeric-mode-threshold` radio, default): comparison operator (`action-numeric-cmp`) + value (`action-numeric-value`); **Top-N** (`action-numeric-mode-topn` radio): order (`action-numeric-order`: top / bottom) + count (`action-numeric-n`). Threshold / Top-N sub-modes encode to `__cmp__:OP:VALUE` / `__top_n__:N:asc|desc` pseudo-patterns for `POST /api/action/bulk-decide`.
  - **Action combo** (`action-action-combo`) — `<select>` with 5 options; wire values: `__keep__` (maps to empty string `""`), `delete`, `__ignore__`, `__lock__`, `__unlock__`.
  - **Live preview** — after any input change, the store calls `previewBulkDecide()` which hits `POST /api/action/bulk-decide` with `preview=true`. The match counter (`action-match-counter`) shows "N of M matched". `action-preview-list` (`<ul>`, a scrolling box) lists every matched path — a preview returns the whole resolved set — so the `action-preview-truncated` "…and N more" line appears only if a response ever carries fewer paths than it matched.
  - **Apply** (`action-btn-apply`) — disabled when `manifest.path === null || actionRunning`. An empty pattern no longer disables Apply (#741, fixing the #397 friction regression): the backend already no-ops on an empty pattern (`core/app_service/action_resolve.py:341` `if not pattern: return []`, pinned by `tests/test_action_resolve.py`), so gating the button on it added friction with no safety value. On click calls `store.applyBulkDecide()`. When `action === "delete"`, the delete-confirm gate (`execute-all-delete-confirm` / `-yes` / `-no` testids) must be accepted before the API call fires. When the server returns 409 `locked_paths`, the inline lock-confirm dialog (`action-lock-confirm-dialog`) appears with three verdicts — see Conditions below (#674).
  - **Recent patterns (#741)** — a "Recent:" jump-menu `<select>` (`action-recent-select`) sits above the Field combo, listing patterns previously Applied for the current field (plus legacy null-field entries, which apply to any field); picking an entry fills the pattern input and the select resets to its placeholder. `action-recent-clear` (shown only when at least one entry is visible for the current field) wipes the whole list across all fields. Entries are recorded after a (non-preview) Apply returns without a 409 lock prompt. Only the 409 stops the recording: any other failed Apply still records the pattern and closes the dialog, so a pattern that never applied can enter Recent. They are persisted under the settings key `ui.action_dialog.recent_patterns` as `[field-or-null, pattern]` tuples, deduped by exact (field, pattern) and capped at 10 entries (enforced on both write and read, so a hand-edited or cross-version settings.json can't render an unbounded list). Numeric fields (`NUMERIC_FIELDS`) are excluded — their `__cmp__:`/`__top_n__:` pseudo-patterns would be meaningless in a re-pickable Recent list.
  - **Pattern-aware delete-confirm (#741)** — when Apply is clicked with `action === "delete"`, the confirm dialog shown is no longer generic "N files will be deleted" copy: it renders a human-readable pattern summary (`action-delete-confirm-summary`, e.g. "File Name contains 'IMG'", or "{field} regex '{pattern}'" for non-Simple-representable patterns) and its confirm button reads "Mark N files for deletion" — deferred-decision wording, since Apply only queues the decision and nothing is deleted until Execute Action runs.
- **Conditions / variants:**
  - `main-action-button` is disabled when no manifest is loaded (`manifest.path === null`).
  - String mode shows both `action-simple-row` and `action-regex-row` side-by-side (no mode toggle).
  - Cheatsheet chips (`action-cheatsheet-{token}`) for `.*`, `\d`, `\w`, `^`, `$`, `\.`, `[abc]` insert tokens into the regex input.
  - The delete-confirm dialog reuses `execute-all-delete-confirm` (same testid set as the execute-dialog all-delete gate) so the flow is consistent across action entry points.
  - **409 locked-rows response (#674):** the inline `action-lock-confirm-dialog` mounts with **three verdicts** — **Cancel** (`action-lock-confirm-btn-cancel`), **Apply to Unlocked Only** (`action-lock-confirm-btn-unlocked-only`), and **Unlock & Apply** (`action-lock-confirm-btn-unlock-apply`). "Unlock & Apply" re-POSTs with `force_locked=true`; "Apply to Unlocked Only" re-POSTs with `skip_locked=true` (the backend applies the decision to the matched-minus-locked subset, computed server-side from the authoritative `is_locked` column, leaving locked rows' `is_locked` + `user_decision` untouched). The 409 body carries `matched_total` so the dialog disables "Apply to Unlocked Only" when every matched row is locked (no unlocked subset) without depending on a stale preview; `force_locked` + `skip_locked` are mutually exclusive (the backend rejects both with 422). This inline dialog is **distinct** from the execute-flow `lock-confirm-dialog` (`LockConfirmDialog`) which is path-scoped — see [Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog).
  - **Column pre-fill from the context menu (#735):** `store.openActionDialog(initialField?)` accepts an optional field-option value; when the row right-click resolves a clicked column (`frontend/src/lib/actionDialogFields.ts`'s `WEB_COL_TO_FIELD`), the dialog opens with that field pre-selected instead of the default "File Name". Mapped columns: name → File Name, similarity → Similarity, action → Action, size → Size (Bytes), shot date → Shot Date; Score and Resolution are intentionally unmapped (open with no pre-fill). An omitted or unrecognised value falls back to the default field, so every pre-#735 argless callsite (toolbar button, menu bar, `ExecuteContextMenu`) is unaffected.
  - **Row-value seed (#893)** — the value half of the same behaviour. Opening the dialog from a highlighted row seeds the matcher with **that row's value for the selected field**, escaped, so the Simple inputs read `("contains", value)` and the pattern matches the row it came from (before #893 every pattern started from an empty box). Seedable fields are **File Name / Folder / Action**. The two other regex fields, Resolution and Lock, are not seeded and open with an empty pattern. The six numeric fields (Size (Bytes), Group Count, Similarity, Score, Creation Date, Shot Date) render the numeric panel, whose `__cmp__:` / `__top_n__:` pseudo-pattern a regex seed cannot express. **Changing the field re-seeds the matcher only while it still holds the previous field's seed (or is empty)** — a pattern the user typed or edited survives a field change (#347). Which row seeds it: the **right-clicked row** from the file-row context menu; **none** from the group-row menu and from the Execute dialog's Select-by entry point; the **highlighted row** for the toolbar button and menu-bar item.
  - **The Simple row follows the regex (#899).** Whenever the pattern changes under it — a hand-edited regex, a cheatsheet chip, a Recent pick — and the new pattern is Simple-representable, the Simple inputs are re-derived from it: hand-typing `^neardup_0` reads back as ("starts with", `neardup_0`), so a following op flip builds on the edit instead of silently replacing it with the stale seed. A pattern Simple cannot represent (a real regex) leaves the Simple inputs as they were, as Qt's `_reverse_parse_to_simple` did. The Simple row's own write-through never re-derives it, so clearing its text keeps the chosen op.
- **Related:** `app/web/routes/action.py` (`POST /api/action/bulk-decide`, now with `skip_locked` + the `matched_total` 409 field); `core/app_service/action_service.py` (`bulk_decide`); `core/app_service/action_resolve.py` (`resolve_matched_paths`); `frontend/src/lib/recentPatterns.ts` (Recent parse/record/visible helpers, #741 sub-item B); `frontend/src/lib/patternSummary.ts` (`buildPatternSummary`, #741 sub-item C); see also [`Set Action dialog — dual-section Simple + Regex view`](#set-action-dialog--dual-section-simple--regex-view) and [`Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog`](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog) (#674 added the Apply-to-Unlocked-Only verdict); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s31_simple_mode_regex.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s31_simple_mode_regex.py) (extended for #741 — empty-pattern Apply-enabled + pattern-summary delete-confirm), [`qa/web/scenarios/s32_lock_confirm_bulk_regex.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s32_lock_confirm_bulk_regex.py) (#674 lock-confirm verdict, asserted via sqlite + GET /api/manifest).
- **Last verified:** 2026-06-22 (Phase 2 web port — s31 scenario added; backend `POST /api/action/bulk-decide` already shipped); 2026-06-23 (web lock-confirm parity #674 — `skip_locked` + 3rd verdict + s32, live-run PASS); 2026-07-03 (#735 — reachable from the file-row and group-row right-click, with column pre-fill; s15 extended); 2026-07-04 (#741 — Apply no longer disabled on an empty pattern, Recent-patterns dropdown, pattern-aware delete-confirm summary; s31 extended)

### Web — main result tree multi-selection

- **Entry point:** File rows in the main result tree (`main-result-tree` → `FileRow`, [frontend/src/components/result/FileRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/FileRow.tsx)) and the row right-click context menu (`context-menu` → [frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx)). Selection state lives in the store's `selection` slice ([frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts](../frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts)).
- **Trigger:** The user left-clicks rows (plain / Ctrl·Cmd / Shift) to build a selection, then right-clicks one to act on it.
- **Behaviour:**
  - **Selection model.** A plain click selects exactly one row (and points the preview pane at it). **Ctrl/Cmd+click** toggles a row in or out of an ordered multi-selection. **Shift+click** extends an inclusive range from the anchor (the last plain click) over the currently-*visible* rows — collapsed groups' rows are excluded from the range. Selected rows get a sky highlight + `aria-selected`; the highlight wins over the Ref-winner tint.
  - **Selection-aware context menu.** The Delete / Keep / Remove-from-list / Lock / Unlock verbs act on the **whole selection** when the right-clicked row is part of it, and on **just that row** otherwise (the file-manager target-resolution rule — a right-click outside the selection also resets the selection to that one row). **Open folder** always targets only the right-clicked row. Batch Delete/Keep decisions and Lock/Unlock issue a single `PATCH /api/decision` / `PATCH /api/lock` for the whole set (optimistic, all-or-nothing revert on failure); Remove-from-list instead finalizes the whole selection via `POST /api/remove` (#694, see below).
  - **Skip finalizes (#694).** The result-tree **Skip** item (labelled "Remove from list" until the 2026-09-18 vocabulary decision; the wire value `ignore` is unchanged) FINALIZES `outcome='ignored'` — the rows leave the review tree immediately (no Execute step). There is NO confirmation dialog on this path; a 409 locked-paths conflict routes through the `LockConfirmDialog` and a resulting singleton triggers the prune offer (`maybeOfferPrune`). Staging a reversible `user_decision='ignore'` instead lives on the per-row decision buttons (`DecisionControl`); the Execute-dialog "Remove from list" remains a single-row finalize behind its own confirm sheet. (Previously this menu only STAGED `ignore` — the [#694](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/694) parity gap, now closed.)
  - **Set Action by Field… + Execute (only selected) on the file-row menu (#735).** A post-Open-Folder group (`ctx-set-action-by-field` / `ctx-execute-selected`); the whole file-row menu reads Keep · Delete · Skip now │ Lock *or* Unlock (whichever the right-clicked row's lock state allows) · Open folder │ Set Action by Field… · Execute Action (only selected)… │ Apply best-copy decisions to this group. "Set Action by Field…" opens the ActionDialog, pre-filled from the clicked column when mapped (see [Web — Action dialog](#web--action-dialog-set-action-by-field)). "Execute Action (only selected)…" invokes the same handler as the menu-bar entry (re-reads the live selection) — see [Web — Execute (only selected)](#web--execute-only-selected).
  - **Selection clears on any manifest mutation.** Loading a manifest, executing, removing, pruning, or a bulk-decide all replace `manifest.groups` and reset the selection so it can never reference vanished rows.
- **Conditions / variants:** There is no group-header *selection* (`GroupRow` stays collapse-only for the multi-select model above — clicking a group header never adds it to `selection.selectedPaths`; since [#709](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/709) it **clears** them, so the highlight always matches what `d`/`k` and the context menu would act on), so a group cannot be selected as one item. However, the group header now has its own **reduced right-click menu** (#735, `GroupRow.tsx` `onContextMenu`) — `ctx-set-action-by-field` (argless, no column context) + `ctx-set-action-remove` (finalizes `outcome='ignored'` for the group's own member paths, same `removeFromList` call as the file-row entry) + `ctx-apply-best-copy` (#744, see [Web — Context menu: Apply best-copy decisions to this group](#web--context-menu-apply-best-copy-decisions-to-this-group)) ONLY. No Keep/Delete, no Lock/Unlock, no Open folder, no Execute-selected — a distinct, narrower affordance from the file-row multi-selection menu above. The single-row scenarios ([s15](../qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py) / [s25](../qa/web/scenarios/s25_empty_area_context_menu.py) / [s35](../qa/web/scenarios/s35_lock_via_context_menu.py)) stay single-target because they right-click a row that was never multi-selected.
- **Related:** See also [Context menu — Set Action (per-file)](#context-menu--set-action-per-file) and [List menu — Remove from List](#list-menu--remove-from-list); [#694](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/694) (result-tree remove now finalizes — resolved); [#735](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/735) (By-Field + Execute-selected file-row entries, group-row reduced menu); [frontend/src/components/ResultTree.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ResultTree.tsx); QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s20_multi_remove_from_list.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s20_multi_remove_from_list.py) (context-menu multi-selection Skip; the menu-bar **List → Skip Selected now** surface has its own driver, [`qa/web/scenarios/s21_list_menu_remove.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s21_list_menu_remove.py)); [`qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py) (#735 file-row + group-row phases).
- **Last verified:** 2026-06-24 (PR-D1 — main-tree multi-selection + selection-aware context menu; #694 — result-tree "Remove from list" now finalizes `outcome='ignored'`, s20 restructured to assert the finalize + live-run PASS); 2026-07-03 (#735 — By-Field + Execute-selected added to the file-row menu, new reduced group-row menu; s15 extended).

### Web — Context menu: Apply best-copy decisions to this group

- **Entry point:** TWO surfaces over one store action (`useAppStore.applyBestCopy`) — (1) the `ctx-apply-best-copy` menu item in BOTH the file-row and group-row context menus ([frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx)), and (2) since layout slice G, a visible **"Keep best · delete rest"** button at the right end of every group header (`group-keep-best-{group}` — deliberately NOT prefixed `row-group-`, which the qa helpers count and traverse the tree by, [frontend/src/components/result/GroupRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/GroupRow.tsx)). Both reach `POST /api/action/apply-best-copy` ([app/web/routes/action.py](../app/web/routes/action.py)) → `core.app_service.action_service.apply_best_copy` ([core/app_service/action_service.py](../core/app_service/action_service.py)).
- **Trigger:** User right-clicks a file row OR a group-header row and picks "Apply best-copy decisions to this group", or clicks the group header's **Keep best · delete rest** button. Acts on the right-clicked row/group's OWN `group_number`, independent of the current multi-selection (`targetPaths`) and independent of file-vs-group variant. **Both surfaces are disabled while the toolbar filter is active** (layout slice TB), with the tooltip "Clear the filter to use Keep best" / 「清除篩選後才能使用保留最佳」 (`web.tree.keep_best_filtered`): the write is server-side and scoped by `group_number`, so under a filter it would mark rows the user cannot see while the group header shows the FILTERED count right beside the button. Disabled rather than re-scoped to the visible rows — picking a "best" copy from a subset is a different decision, and a silently wrong one. Open questions Q5 promoted the action onto the header because it is the fast path through the entire screen and «a right-click action is invisible to the users who most need it».
- **Behaviour:** Within the target group, the highest-scoring row becomes the keeper (`user_decision=""` + `is_locked=true`); every OTHER row the classifier positively identified as a duplicate (`action` in `EXACT` / `REVIEW_DUPLICATE`) gets `user_decision="delete"`. A Ref-tier row pulled into the group by the ungated filename pair edge (RAW+JPG same-stem, Live-Photo MOV passenger) is left untouched — same allowlist `core.services.auto_select.non_keepers_for_aggressive_delete` uses for the scan-time aggressive auto-select path (see [Scan dialog — auto-select aggressive](#scan-dialog--auto-select-aggressive-delete-all-others)). No optimistic client-side update — the store swaps in the authoritative `groups` array from the response. A locked row in the write-set (keeper or delete-target) behaves differently per entry point, deliberately. From the **context menu** it triggers the SAME `LockConfirmDialog` used by decision/execute/remove (409 `locked_paths`, non-destructive "decision-only" copy variant) — "Unlock & Apply" force-unlocks previously-locked delete-targets in the same write (the keeper's own lock write is unaffected); "Unlocked Only" narrows the write to the unlocked subset. From the **header button** there is no dialog at all: it sends `skip_locked`, so a locked row keeps BOTH its decision and its lock and the write silently narrows to the unlocked subset — Q5: «it must never override a lock, and it must say so when it skips one».
- **Behaviour — the undo toast (layout slice G, Q5):** the header button has **no confirm step**, because «the button does not delete anything … a confirm dialog in front of a reversible, non-destructive action is the classic way to teach people to dismiss dialogs without reading, which then costs you at Execute, where the dialog matters». Instead, a successful apply-best-copy from **either** entry point raises a **single-slot toast** above the status bar ([frontend/src/components/Toast.tsx](../frontend/src/components/Toast.tsx), `main-toast`, `role="status"`): *"Group 12 · 4 files marked for deletion · Undo"* / 「群組 12 · 已標記 4 個檔案待刪除 · 復原」, plus *"1 locked file unchanged"* / 「1 個鎖定的檔案未變更」 when it skipped one. Both counts are derived client-side — the response carries only `matched` (keeper + deletes as one number) and no locked count. The locked count reports only rows the write could actually have targeted (classification `EXACT` / `REVIEW_DUPLICATE`), so a lock on the keeper or on a Ref-tier passenger — neither of which was ever a delete-target — is not reported as "skipped". It dismisses after **~6 s**, with the timer **paused while hovered or focused** and resumed with the REMAINDER, because six seconds is enough to read a sentence but not to read it, decide and aim. **Single slot:** a keep-best on a second group REPLACES the toast and restarts the clock, so Undo always reverses exactly the latest action and never a half-remembered stack. **A repeat on the SAME group is handled apart from that**, because it is the one case where replacing the snapshot destroys the undo: a second apply reads an ALREADY-APPLIED group as its "before", so snapshotting that would make Undo restore the damage. Both entry points therefore disable themselves while that group's write is in flight (`keepBestPending`), and when a later toast does land on the group the standing toast already describes, the EARLIER recording of each path wins. **Undo** restores each touched row's prior `user_decision` **and** its prior lock, from a snapshot taken before the write — the decision PATCH goes out with `force_locked` because apply-best-copy locks the keeper it chooses, so a decision-only undo would be refused by the very lock the action had just created. A failed undo leaves the toast up rather than stranding the user with an unreversible write.
- **Conditions / variants:** Deliberately NO `match_confidence` filtering — that field lives only on the scanner's transient `ManifestRow` shape and is never persisted to the manifest DB, so `core.models.PhotoRecord` (what the endpoint reads) has no such attribute; the shared helper's confidence check is naturally always-true here (owner-accepted #744 scope decision, not a gap). A group with no scored rows (e.g. every member is a passenger) is a benign no-op. **History:** an earlier version of this right-click action was removed in [PR #224](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/224) (closes [#210](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/210)) in favour of the Set Action dialog's Top-N-by-Score numeric condition, which the web dialog still offers (see [Set Action dialog — numeric comparison panel](#set-action-dialog--numeric-comparison-panel)); #744 brought the one-click action back.
- **Related:** [#744](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/744); design contract `docs/design/web-port-tech-design.md:2265`; `core/services/auto_select.py` (`top_score_path_per_group`, `build_auto_select_writes`, `non_keepers_for_aggressive_delete` — reused, not reimplemented); `frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts` (`applyBestCopy`); `frontend/src/components/dialogs/LockConfirmDialog.tsx` (`op="apply-best-copy"`); backend tests [`tests/test_web_action_routes.py::TestApplyBestCopy`](../tests/test_web_action_routes.py); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s72_apply_best_copy.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s72_apply_best_copy.py) (plain scan → group-row right-click → assert keeper/delete split via GET /api/manifest → reload + reopen via picker → assert durability) and [`qa/web/scenarios/s75_keep_best_button.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s75_keep_best_button.py) (the header button: lock one duplicate → click → assert the locked row keeps its decision AND its lock while the others are marked → read the toast in both locales → Undo → assert the group is byte-identical to its pre-button state, keeper's lock included). Layout slice G: `frontend/src/components/Toast.tsx`, `frontend/src/lib/groupSummary.ts`, open questions Q4/Q5.
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-18 (layout slice G — s75 live-run on `qa/sandbox/near-duplicates/`: the header button left the locked `EXACT` duplicate at `user_decision=''` + `is_locked=True` while the other three went to `delete` and the keeper took `''` + lock; toast read `Group 1 · 3 files marked for deletion · 1 locked file unchanged · Undo` and 「群組 1 · 已標記 3 個檔案待刪除 · 1 個鎖定的檔案未變更 · 復原」; Undo restored the group byte-identically, the keeper's lock included. s72 green on the same build); 2026-07-11 (#744 — initial implementation, reintroducing the action PR #224 had removed)

### Web — Context menu: stays inside the window (#897)

- **Entry point:** The result-tree row context menu, both variants — [frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.tsx) (`context-menu`) — and the Execute dialog's row menu ([frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx](../frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.tsx)). Both measure through [frontend/src/hooks/useMenuPlacement.ts](../frontend/src/hooks/useMenuPlacement.ts) and are placed by the pure [frontend/src/lib/menuPlacement.ts](../frontend/src/lib/menuPlacement.ts) `placeMenu`.
- **Trigger:** Right-clicking a file row or group header anywhere in the window, including near its bottom or right edge, or in a short window.
- **Behaviour:** The menu opens below-right of the cursor when it fits, as before. When it would overflow the bottom it **flips above** the cursor (its bottom edge at the cursor, as Qt's `QMenu` did); when it would overflow the right it flips left. When neither side of the cursor has room it **slides** in until its far edge is 4px inside the window. When it is **taller than the window** it is pinned 4px from the top with `max-height: 100vh − 8px` and **scrolls internally**, so every item — the last one included — can still be reached. The menu is measured in a layout effect and re-placed before the browser paints, so it is never drawn off-screen first.
- **Conditions / variants:** Previously the menu was drawn at the cursor unconditionally (`style={{ left: x, top: y }}`): in a 400px-tall window its lower items sat past the viewport edge and could not be clicked, and s73 had to widen its window to work around it. The box carries `w-max` so a menu opened near the right edge measures at its real width instead of shrink-wrapping (and so wrapping its labels) before it is moved. The Execute dialog's row menu is `absolute` inside a layer over the dialog (to stay clickable inside the Radix modal), so its coordinates are layer-relative: it is clamped to the **window**, not the dialog — the dialog deliberately does not clip it (s30) — by converting through the layer's on-screen origin and back.
- **Related:** issue [#897](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/897); unit tests `frontend/src/lib/menuPlacement.test.ts` (flip / slide / scroll math), `frontend/src/components/ContextMenu.test.tsx` and `frontend/src/components/execute/ExecuteContextMenu.test.tsx` (each component applies it; the latter through a dialog-offset layer); QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py) step (g) — at 1280×400, right-click the last row and hit-test the menu's last item.
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#897 — unit tests; s15 step (g) in CI `web-scenario-batch`).

### Web — Execute (only selected)

- **Entry point:** Menu bar **Action → "Execute Action (only selected)…"** (`menu-action-execute-selected` → [frontend/src/components/MenuBar.tsx](../frontend/src/components/MenuBar.tsx)) — the label uses the full "Execute Action…" form, which the right-click entry already used (copy audit M2), wired from [frontend/src/App.tsx](../frontend/src/App.tsx) to the store's `openExecuteDialog(scopePaths)` ([frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts](../frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts)). **Second entry point (#735):** the result-tree file-row context menu's `ctx-execute-selected` item ("Execute Action (only selected)…") invokes the identical handler — a *distinct DOM element* from the menu-bar entry, both calling the same `handleExecuteSelectedOnly` in `App.tsx`. Absent from the group-row reduced menu (group-only right-clicks stay on the menu-bar path).
- **Trigger:** From the menu bar, the entry requires ≥1 row already selected (cluster-D1 multi-selection) and is **disabled** otherwise. From a file row's context menu the entry has no separate gate — right-clicking a file row always establishes at least that one row as the selection first (the target-resolution rule, see [Web — main result tree multi-selection](#web--main-result-tree-multi-selection)), so a non-empty selection is guaranteed by the time the item is clickable.
- **Behaviour:**
  - **Gating.** The entry is disabled unless a manifest is loaded **and** the main-tree selection is non-empty. The unscoped **Execute Action…** entry (and toolbar Execute button) stay always-available and open the dialog over every decided row.
  - **Group-pull scope (#430).** Opening it snapshots the current selection and scopes the execute dialog to the selected rows' **whole groups** — selecting any one row of a group pulls that group's rows into the dialog (so peer/ref rows stay visible while triaging). The scope is stored as `execute.scopeGroupNumbers` (a set of group numbers, robust across manifest refresh) and confines **every** groups-derived view: the tree, the all-delete + hidden-destructive banners, and the Execute commit. Out-of-scope groups are not shown and not executed.
  - **Static label.** The Execute button keeps the constant "Execute" / "Executing…" label — there is no scoped-selection label variant ([#410](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/410) retired the "Execute Action (highlighted)" wording).
  - **Scope resets** to null (unscoped) when the dialog closes, and the selection itself clears on the executing manifest mutation.
- **Conditions / variants:** The scope is fixed before the dialog opens (menu-gated, selection-scoped). It is distinct from the dialog's own **Execute selected**, which acts on a selection made inside the Execute tree — that tree has its own Ctrl/Cmd-toggle and Shift-range multi-selection, independent of the main tree's ([#697](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/697); see [Execute Action — partial execution via "Execute selected"](#execute-action--partial-execution-via-execute-selected)).
- **Related:** See also [Execute Action — scope to selected groups](#execute-action--scope-to-selected-groups-only-selected-entry-410-430-429) ([#430](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/430) group-pull, [#410](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/410) static label); builds on Web — main result tree multi-selection; [#735](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/735) (context-menu entry point); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s44_execute_selected_only.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s44_execute_selected_only.py), [`qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s15_context_menu.py) (#735 context-menu phase).
- **Last verified:** 2026-06-24 (PR-D2 — Execute (only selected) menu entry + group-pull scope; s44 live-run PASS; s54/s60/s64/s20 regressions PASS); 2026-07-03 (#735 — file-row + group-row context-menu parity: By-Field pre-fill, context-menu Execute-selected entry, reduced group-row menu; s15 extended).

### Web — ScanDialog source/output persistence

- **Entry point:** The Scan dialog ([frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx)) reads/writes the allowlisted `sources.list` / `sources.output` keys via `GET` / `PATCH /api/settings` ([app/web/routes/settings.py](../app/web/routes/settings.py)).
- **Trigger:** Automatic — the source list + output path are loaded when the dialog opens and saved when the user clicks **Start Scan**.
- **Behaviour:**
  - **Load on open.** Opening the dialog fetches `GET /api/settings` and rebuilds the source rows from the persisted `sources.list` (`[{path, recursive}, …]`, label derived from each path's basename) and the output field from `sources.output`. A blank row + empty output show synchronously first; persisted values override only when present, so a freshly-typed row is never clobbered by an empty response. Malformed persisted entries (non-dict, missing `path`) are skipped.
  - **Save on Start Scan.** Clicking **Start Scan** fires `PATCH /api/settings` with the current `sources.list` (path + per-row recursive flag) and `sources.output` **before** the scan starts. The save is fire-and-forget and non-fatal: a settings-save failure never blocks the scan.
- **Near-duplicate threshold floor ([#823](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/823)):** the pHash and dHash number inputs render `min="2"` and their labels read **range: 2–20** — see also [Scan dialog — collapse Advanced Settings](#scan-dialog--collapse-advanced-settings). `min` is only a browser hint, so the client-side guard is `clampThresholdInput` at request-build time: a typed `1` still leaves as `threshold: 2` on `POST /api/scan`. Since [#876](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/876) the **authoritative** guard is the server — `WebScanRequest` bounds both hash thresholds to 2–20 and answers 422 below that — so the clamp is what keeps this dialog honest, not what keeps a bad value out of `classify`. **The field also snaps to the clamped value** — on blur and again when **Start Scan** is clicked — so what the user sees is what actually runs. Without that, `min` merely set `rangeUnderflow` and left `1` on screen while the scan ran at 2, which is the same dishonesty this ticket exists to remove. The snap deliberately does **not** fire per keystroke: clamping mid-type corrupts a clear-and-retype. Mean-colour keeps its existing behaviour (its floor is 0, so only a typed negative underflows) — a known asymmetry, not an oversight. A localized helper line under each input (`web.scan.threshold_parity_note`, `data-testid` `scan-phash-parity-note` / `scan-dhash-parity-note`) explains that photographic pHash distances are always even, so an odd value usually matches the same files as the even value below it, and that 2 is the strictest useful setting. The mean-colour input keeps `min="0"`. **Dialog height:** adding those two helper lines pushed **Start Scan** off the bottom of a 1280×800 viewport (the expanded dialog was already 807px tall in an 800px window, and the shared `DialogContent` is a centred box with no height cap), so this dialog is now capped at `max-h-[90vh]` with `overflow-y-auto`: with Advanced expanded the panel scrolls internally instead of growing past the window edge, and every control including **Start Scan** stays reachable. The shared `DialogContent` itself stays uncapped; the Execute dialog (`max-h-[90vh]`) and the delete confirm with its row list (`max-h-[85vh]`) carry caps of their own.
- **Dialog copy is catalog-driven (copy audit SC1/SC3/SC5, 2026-09-14):** this was the client's largest untranslated surface — the title, the "Sources" / "Output path" / "Advanced settings" / "Grouping sensitivity" labels, the three threshold labels + their short descriptions + their three long hover tooltips, every checkbox label, the whole source row (Label / Path placeholders, the Recursive label, and every `aria-label`), the error line and **Start Scan** were all hardcoded English, so a zh_TW user opened an entirely English dialog. Every one now resolves through `web.scan.*` in both catalogs. The English wording is unchanged (the defect was the missing `t()`, not the words); the `*_desc` lines deliberately omit a trailing "2 is the strictest useful setting" clause, because the `web.scan.threshold_parity_note` paragraph below each input already says it (copy audit SC2 — the redundancy, not a bug).
- **Conditions / variants:** The source list, the output path and the **Auto-tune reader concurrency** checkbox persist — the checkbox as `ui.scan_dialog.autotune_read_knee`, read when the dialog opens and written on **Start Scan** (see [Scan dialog — read-knee autotune opt-out](#scan-dialog--read-knee-autotune-opt-out)). pHash/dHash/mean-color thresholds are **not** persisted (#736 — the Advanced settings' "Grouping sensitivity" number inputs are sent per-scan in the scan request only, never through `patchSettings`, and the `/api/settings` allowlist excludes the threshold keys) — pinned by s23b. The auto-select and aggressive checkboxes are likewise sent per-scan in the scan request and are not persisted. The source list itself renders case-insensitively **alphabetized by path** (display-only, #736; the rule dates from [#213](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/213)/[#223](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/pull/223)) — while the underlying array stays insertion-ordered, so dedup's source-priority inference (top-of-scan = highest priority, `scanner/dedup.py`) and each row's testid `idx` are unaffected. Per-source **labels** are not durable — only `{path, recursive}` is stored, so a reopened dialog re-derives each row's label from the path basename. The async load fills only a still-pristine dialog, so reopening it while typing never clobbers in-progress edits.
- **Related:** See also [Scan dialog — multi-source scan](#scan-dialog--multi-source-scan) and [Scan dialog — folder list (no priority arrows)](#scan-dialog--folder-list-no-priority-arrows); part of the web-port FE-parity backlog [#678](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/678) (cluster E); [#736](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/736) (grouping-sensitivity thresholds + alphabetized source list); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s23a_set_settings.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s23a_set_settings.py) (write side) + [`qa/web/scenarios/s23b_verify_settings.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s23b_verify_settings.py) (reload round-trip) + [`qa/web/scenarios/s17_scan_dialog_widgets.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s17_scan_dialog_widgets.py) (#736 threshold wiring + alphabetized-display proof; #823 near-duplicate floor + parity note).
- **Last verified:** 2026-06-24 (cluster E — ScanDialog source persistence; s23a/s23b live-run PASS via batch; full 50-scenario batch PASS confirming per-scenario reset isolation); 2026-07-03 (#736 — added grouping-sensitivity threshold inputs + display-only alphabetized source list; s17 extended); 2026-09-07 (#823 — `min="2"` + clamp-to-2 on the wire + parity note; s17 live-run PASS).

### Web — scan dialog: subfolders by default, empty result stays open

- **Entry point:** [frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx) — `blankSource()` / `sourcesFromSettings()` (the per-row **Recursive** checkbox) and the scan-finished effect + `scan-empty-message` line.
- **Trigger:** Opening the Scan dialog or adding a source row; a scan that ends with the `completed_empty` SSE event (the walk found no media files).
- **Behaviour:** Qt parity on two points (#896).
  - **Recursive defaults on.** A new source row's Recursive checkbox (`web.scan.recursive`, "包含子資料夾" in zh_TW) starts **checked**, as Qt's `_SourceListWidget.add_entry(recursive=True)` did. With it unchecked, a folder whose photos all sit in subfolders scanned 0 files. A persisted source keeps its saved flag, so a box the user unticked stays unticked; a persisted entry with no flag loads checked, like Qt's `resolve_source_entries`.
  - **An empty scan keeps the dialog open.** On `completed_empty`, the dialog stays open with **"No media files found — nothing to scan."** (`web.scan.empty_result`, `role="status"`) and Start Scan enabled, so the user can fix the sources (for example tick Recursive) and retry, as Qt's `_on_completed_empty` did. Before, the dialog closed and the status bar went straight back to "Ready" with nothing to say why no manifest loaded. No manifest is written or loaded. Closing the dialog resets the scan, so the next open reloads the persisted sources and shows no stale message. A scan that writes a manifest still closes the dialog and loads it, unchanged.
- **Conditions / variants:** The status bar still reads "Ready" after an empty scan; the message lives in the dialog only. The per-source flag is still persisted on every Start Scan (see [Web — ScanDialog source/output persistence](#web--scandialog-sourceoutput-persistence)).
- **Related:** [#896](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/896) (the nested-folder "scan hangs" report this explains). Vitest `frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.test.tsx` (default checked, missing-flag load, empty result stays open + message + Start enabled, closing resets, normal finish still closes); web scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s02_empty_folder.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s02_empty_folder.py).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#896)

### Web — app shell never scrolls; the tree does (#894)

- **Entry point:** The root element of the app shell ([frontend/src/App.tsx](../frontend/src/App.tsx)) — `h-screen overflow-hidden flex flex-col` wrapping MenuBar, the header toolbar, `<main>` and the footer status bar.
- **Trigger:** Browsing a loaded manifest — selecting a row, scrolling the result list, resizing the window.
- **Behaviour:** The document is exactly the viewport's height and is **never scrollable**. The MenuBar, toolbar and status bar stay in place at all times; every scroll happens inside the pane that owns it (the result tree's own scroll container, the preview pane's media area, a group grid). This is what lets the sticky column header ([#685](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/685)) stay pinned — it can only pin to the tree's own scroller.
- **Conditions / variants:** Previously the root carried `min-h-screen` — a *minimum* height, which leaves the column's main size indefinite, so `<main>`'s `flex-1` (`flex-basis: 0%`) fell back to its **content** height and `ResultTree`'s `h-full` fell back to `auto`. The visible trigger was **selecting a row**, not a long list: the result tree is `contain: strict` (size-contained), so even ~4900px of virtualised rows contribute nothing to layout — only the preview pane's min-content height could push the page, and the preview pane is empty until a row is picked. Measured on the pre-fix build at a 620×400 window with a row selected: the root grew to **571px against a 400px viewport**, and scrolling put the toolbar at **y = −138** (off-screen). Same steps after the fix: 400/400, `scrollY` pinned at 0, toolbar at y = 33. At 1280×720 the pre-fix build looked fine, which is why the defect reached the owner's trial.
- **Related:** issue [#894](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/894); [#685](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/685) sticky column header; [#699](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/699) / [#709](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/709) scroll-into-view geometry. QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s73_action_seed_and_page_scroll.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s73_action_seed_and_page_scroll.py) (asserts page-height ≤ viewport, tree `scrollHeight > clientHeight`, and that `window.scrollTo` cannot move the toolbar).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-12 (#894 — live Playwright measurement before and after on the same fixture; s73 passes on the fixed build).

### Web — result-tree column sort and resize persistence

- **Entry point:** The main result tree ([frontend/src/components/ResultTree.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ResultTree.tsx)) gained a sticky column-header row ([frontend/src/components/result/ColumnHeaderRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/ColumnHeaderRow.tsx)) with clickable **File Name** / **Size** sort cells and a right-edge resize handle on every metadata column. The column registry + sort comparators live in [frontend/src/lib/resultColumns.ts](../frontend/src/lib/resultColumns.ts).
- **Trigger:** User clicks a sortable column header (sort) or drags a column's right-edge resize handle (width).
- **Behaviour:**
  - **Sort.** The default is server response order (no sort) — so every existing row-reading scenario is unaffected. Clicking **File Name** sorts each group's rows case-folded ascending; a repeat click toggles to descending; clicking **Size** sorts numerically on the raw byte count (not the human-readable label). File Name + Size sort this way; **Action** (since [#923](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/923)) is the third sortable column with its own cycle — see [Web — sort by decision](#web--sort-by-decision-action-header). Sort is applied **per group** (group order itself is unchanged). The chosen sort is held in the `resultView` store slice, so it survives an in-session manifest reopen (re-loading the same `.db` without a page reload).
  - **Resize + persist.** Dragging a column's right-edge handle resizes it (floor 40px); the new width is written to `localStorage` (`pm.result-tree.column-widths.v1`) and restored after a `page.reload()` and, in a browser tab, on the next launch. In the packaged app the widths are expected to reset on every relaunch, because its pywebview window runs in private mode (code-read, not live-verified; tracked as [#944](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/944)). localStorage is per browser; widths hydrate at store creation so a manifest load never resets them. **End of drag (#796):** the drag ends — one persisted write, window listeners removed — on `mouseup`, on window `blur`, on `pointercancel`, or on the first `mousemove` that arrives with no button held. The last three cover a button released where the page never hears the `mouseup` (over another application, or a system-cancelled pointer); before that the column kept resizing on every later mouse move over the page.
  - **Header height feeds the virtualizer (#699).** The header is a normal-flow sibling **above** the virtualizer's spacer inside the same scroll container, so the row list starts one header-height into the container's content. `ResultTree` measures the header's rendered height at runtime (a ref on `ColumnHeaderRow`'s root plus a layout effect and a `ResizeObserver`, so a late font load or a taller translated label re-measures) and passes it to `useVirtualizer` as `scrollMargin`; each row is then positioned at `start - scrollMargin` inside the spacer, so a row's on-screen position is unchanged while the virtualizer's coordinates finally equal the scroll container's own. Nothing about the rendered page changes — the value it fixes is the windowing math (which rows are mounted near a scroll boundary, and where `scrollToIndex` lands). The measured value is mirrored onto the tree root as `data-scroll-margin` so the coordinate contract is observable: `translateY + data-scroll-margin` must equal the row's offset from the container's content top.
- **Conditions / variants:** Sort persistence is **in-session only** (cross-launch sort is explicitly out of scope; s45 pins the in-session half); width persistence survives a reload and, in a browser tab, a relaunch — but in the packaged app, whose pywebview window runs in private mode, it is expected not to survive a relaunch (code-read, not live-verified; tracked as [#944](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/944)). No backend change — sort/resize is a pure client proxy-model concern; `GET /api/manifest` order stays the service-layer default. Header labels resolve from the `web.column.*` i18n catalog (so they translate in zh_TW); the Size header reads **"Size"** (human-readable), while the Set Action dialog's field of the same data is still named **"Size (Bytes)"**. The `scrollMargin` rule above is **measured, never a constant** — the header renders 28px since layout slice C (~25px before it), and a font/padding/i18n change moves it again, so a hardcoded number would silently reintroduce the offset. The same measurement also drives **column shedding** (slice C): the header's box width IS the table's available width, so one `ResizeObserver` feeds both the virtualizer's `scrollMargin` and the column budget — see [Web — Daylight theme and results-tree framing](#web--daylight-theme-and-results-tree-framing) § Columns.
- **Related:** See also [Main window — sort persistence within session](#main-window--sort-persistence-within-session) and [Main window — column order/width persistence](#main-window--column-orderwidth-persistence); part of the web-port FE-parity backlog [#678](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/678) ([#685](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/685)); sticky-header virtualizer offset + tall-manifest scroll coverage [#699](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/699); QA scenarios [`qa/web/scenarios/s45_sort_persistence.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s45_sort_persistence.py) (sort + in-session persistence) + [`qa/web/scenarios/s47_column_layout_persist.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s47_column_layout_persist.py) (width persist across reload — since layout slice C File Name is the FILL column, so the driver asserts the **stored** width, published as `data-col-basis`, moves by exactly the drag distance and survives the reload, plus the rendered box never falling below it; **#699 phase**: a synthetic 30-group / 60-row manifest, the only web fixture taller than the viewport — scrolls the tree and asserts the header stays pinned, the first row is not occluded, and every rendered row sits exactly where the virtualizer's coordinate says).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-05 (#699 — `scrollMargin` from the measured header height; s47 live-run on the pre-fix build FAILS with a 25.00px row-coordinate offset at scrollTop 0, mid-scroll and back at top, and PASSES with 0.00px after; s01/s15/s25/s26/s40/s45/s47/s49/s68 batch 9/9 PASS. Earlier: 2026-06-24 #685 — result-tree column model; full 52-scenario batch PASS confirming the default-order isolation held across the 21 row-reading scenarios; 279 vitest).

### Web — sort by decision (Action header)

- **Entry point:** The **Action** column header in the result tree ([ColumnHeaderRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/ColumnHeaderRow.tsx)) is the canonical control. **View → Sort by action** in the menu bar ([MenuBar.tsx](../frontend/src/components/MenuBar.tsx)) mirrors it with **Keep first**, **Delete first** and **Clear sort**. The order and its rules live in [lib/decisionSort.ts](../frontend/src/lib/decisionSort.ts); the state lives in the `resultView` store slice.
- **Trigger:** The user clicks the Action header, or picks an entry in View → Sort by action.
- **Behaviour:**
  - **The cycle.** A click on the Action header goes to **Keep first**, a second click to **Delete first**, and a third clears the sort.
    - Keep first orders **Keep → Skip → Delete**; Delete first is the reverse.
    - A cleared sort returns every group to its default order: the server's, which is score-descending.
    - The table has **one** sort state. Picking Action replaces a File Name / Size sort and vice versa.
    - The sort applies per group, to all groups at once. Groups keep their order and the group header stays put.
  - **Ties keep the server order.** Rows with the same decision stay in the order `GET /api/manifest` sent them, which is score-descending by default (the user's `sorting.defaults` can change that). Owner decision: there is no separate score tie key, so a configured default is honoured. This is also what satisfies [#743](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/743)'s within-group secondary sort. **Lock is not a sort key**: a locked row sorts by its decision.
  - **The order is spelled.** ▾/▴ means little on a three-value enum, so while the Action sort is active the header reads **ACTION · KEEP FIRST** / **ACTION · DELETE FIRST** (「動作 · 保留優先」 / 「動作 · 刪除優先」). The sub-label truncates before the field name does. The View menu ticks the active entry, and **Clear sort** is disabled while no sort is active.
  - **A decision change never moves a row** (design F3's deferred re-sort). Rows are ordered by the decisions captured when the sort was applied, not by the live ones. So staging Delete on the top Keep row leaves it where it is, instead of pulling the next row under a pointer that is still moving toward an irreversible verb.
    - The header shows a **4px accent (`#a85a2c`) dot** right of ▴/▾, with the tooltip "Order is out of date — click to re-sort" / 「排序已過期 — 點擊重新排序」.
    - The next **click on the header** (or re-picking the ticked menu entry) re-sorts in place **without advancing the cycle**. The rows slide to their new places over 120ms ease-out (skipped under `prefers-reduced-motion`), and the keyboard cursor stays on its row.
    - A **group collapse/expand**, a **filter change** or a **density change** re-sorts silently: the dot clears and nothing animates.
- **Conditions / variants:** The Action sort is **not persisted**, neither across launches nor in `localStorage`; File Name / Size persistence is unchanged. The dot appears only when some row's live decision differs from the one the order was built from, so changing a decision back clears it. Opening or re-opening a manifest (including a scan's auto-load) keeps an active Action sort but starts it fresh: the loaded rows are ordered by their decisions at load time, with no stale dot, and a decision change after that is deferred as above. Owner rulings of 2026-09-29 depart from design F3 and are recorded to design as "FEEDBACK - F1 F3 implementation deviations":
  - the mirror lives in the **View menu**, not the toolbar (the toolbar strip has no width left at 1280px);
  - the buckets are **Keep / Skip / Delete**, because the #584 model has no "undecided" state (`''` *is* keep);
  - the sub-label words are "Keep first" / "Delete first".
- **Related:** [#923](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/923) (design ruling F3, `REPLY - review screen post-implementation.md`); [#743](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/743) (secondary sort); QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s79_decision_sort.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s79_decision_sort.py); unit tests `frontend/src/lib/decisionSort.test.ts`, `frontend/src/store/decisionSort.store.test.ts`, `frontend/src/components/ResultTree.decisionSort.test.tsx`.
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-30 (#923 — vitest; s79 in CI's `web-scenario-batch`).

### Web — manifest decision persistence

- **Entry point:** Any per-row decision in the main result tree — the `DecisionControl` button row (three direct-click buttons **Keep / Delete / Skip** — 保留 / 刪除 / 略過 — for the wire values `""` / `"delete"` / `"ignore"`; one click stages the decision with no menu to open, #744; each segment carries the long form in its tooltip) ([frontend/src/components/result/DecisionControl.tsx](../frontend/src/components/result/DecisionControl.tsx)) or the right-click **Set Action** items — calls `store.setDecision` → `PATCH /api/decision` → `ManifestRepository.batch_update_decisions` ([infrastructure/manifest_repository.py](../infrastructure/manifest_repository.py)).
- **Trigger:** Automatic — every staged keep/delete/ignore decision is persisted the instant it is made.
- **Behaviour:** The web app has **no explicit Save step and no dirty state**. `PATCH /api/decision` writes `user_decision` to the `migration_manifest` table synchronously (`executemany` + `commit()` + `close()`), and `GET /api/manifest` is stateless — it re-reads the DB on every request ("SQLite is the only source of truth"). A decision therefore survives a full page reload + manifest re-open with no user action: the Zustand store is wiped on reload, yet re-opening the same `.db` re-hydrates the decision from disk. This delivers, by construction, the **guarantee** an explicit Save step would exist for (don't lose review work) — see [Save Manifest Decisions — base flow](#save-manifest-decisions--base-flow).
- **Conditions / variants:** There is deliberately **no** **File → Save Manifest Decisions…** item in the menu bar ([frontend/src/components/MenuBar.tsx](../frontend/src/components/MenuBar.tsx)): a web "Save" button would only WAL-checkpoint the already-committed DB, so it would be a semantically-hollow affordance that teaches a false "click Save or lose work" model. The backend `POST /api/save` (a WAL-checkpoint / copy-to-new-path utility — `execute_service.py` `save_manifest()`) exists and is store-plumbed but has no UI entry point. The **save-as / export-snapshot** flow (`POST /api/save` with a `target_path`) is the one genuine open product question — tracked as a deferred follow-up, not shipped here. Since [#733](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/733), `PATCH /api/decision` is lock-gated: targeting a locked row returns 409 `locked_paths` unless `force_locked`/`skip_locked` is set (see [Execute Action — lock-confirm dialog](#execute-action--lock-confirm-dialog) → Web parity).
- **Related:** See also [Save Manifest Decisions — base flow](#save-manifest-decisions--base-flow); web-port menu-bar parity backlog [#673](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/673) (Save-Manifest sub-item); QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s12_save_manifest.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s12_save_manifest.py) (durability across reload + reopen + on-disk commit).
- **Last verified:** 2026-06-28 (s12 web port — live-run PASS; decision round-trips through reload + reopen and is committed on disk).

### Web — scan dialog invalid source path

- **Entry point:** The scan dialog's source-path field ([frontend/src/components/scan/SourceRow.tsx](../frontend/src/components/scan/SourceRow.tsx)) + **Start Scan** ([frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx)).
- **Trigger:** Starting a scan whose source path does not exist. (A path that exists but is a **file** is not caught here: the walker only checks existence, the directory listing of a file fails, is logged as an unreadable directory and skipped, so that source contributes no files.)
- **Behaviour:** The web does **not** validate the source path before the scan (no pre-scan existence check on the field). A non-existent path is accepted by `POST /api/scan`; the scanner's `scan_sources` then raises `FileNotFoundError: Source directory not found: <path>` ([scanner/walker.py](../scanner/walker.py)), which the SSE stream emits as a `failed` event. The store sets `scan.status='failed'` + `scan.error` ([frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts](../frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts)) and `ScanDialog` renders a `role="alert"` paragraph that **names the offending path** — and **stays open** so the user can correct it. Fixing the path and re-scanning clears the alert and proceeds. This realises the #144 guarantee (a bad path is never a silent no-op) through the scan-failure surface; the retired desktop client rejected the path up front, when it was added.
- **Conditions / variants:** Validation happens when the scan runs, not when the path is typed (the retired desktop client blocked it on **+ Add**, before any scan). The exact-parity pre-scan inline validation (a `GET /api/fs/stat` probe) is a deferred enhancement, tracked as [#678](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/678) item C — declined here to avoid a filesystem-information-disclosure endpoint for a timing-only nicety. The desktop's native "Save Manifest As" output dialog (#216) has no web analog: the output **Browse…** opens the in-DOM `FsBrowser` save-mode picker (covered by s17).
- **Related:** QA scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s38_scan_dialog_invalid_path.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s38_scan_dialog_invalid_path.py); feature-parity backlog [#678](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/678) (item C — inline path validation).
- **Last verified:** 2026-06-29 (s38 web port — live-run PASS).

### Web — scan progress throughput / ETA / localized stages (#740)

- **Entry point:** The running-state panel inside the scan dialog ([frontend/src/components/scan/ScanProgress.tsx](../frontend/src/components/scan/ScanProgress.tsx)), fed by the SSE `stage` event's `files_per_sec` field (already captured in `ScanState.filesPerSec`, [frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts](../frontend/src/store/useAppStore.ts)) and a receiver-computed ETA ([frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/ScanDialog.tsx)). The backend half (stage emission and the throughput window) is under [Scan dialog — stage / throughput / ETA progress (#424)](#scan-dialog--stage--throughput--eta-progress-424).
- **Trigger:** Automatic while a scan is running — every SSE `stage` event re-renders the panel.
- **Behaviour:**
  - **Localized stage labels.** The raw `stage_name` the backend emits (`WALK`/`HASH`/`EXIFTOOL`/`CLASSIFY`/`SCORE`/`WRITE` — the `core/app_service/scan_runner.py` `STAGE_*` constants) is mapped to a localized label via new `web.scan.stage_*` i18n keys ([translations/en.yml](../translations/en.yml) / [translations/zh_TW.yml](../translations/zh_TW.yml)). The lookup lives in [frontend/src/lib/scanProgress.ts](../frontend/src/lib/scanProgress.ts) (`stageLabel`) and is used at **both** call sites that previously rendered the raw stage id: the scan dialog's status line and the main-window status bar's "Scanning — …" text ([frontend/src/App.tsx](../frontend/src/App.tsx)). An unrecognised stage name (a future backend stage this build predates) falls back to the raw id, and an empty stage name falls back to "Running…" — the pre-#740 behaviour — rather than crashing or rendering a blank line.
  - **Throughput.** The dialog shows a compact "N files/sec" line (`formatThroughput`, `scanProgress.ts`) — one decimal below 10/s, whole numbers at/above. Hidden entirely (not shown as "—") when the rate is 0 or undefined, e.g. before the first sample.
  - **ETA.** Computed receiver-side as `remaining / files_per_sec` (`formatEta`) and gated by the #424 rule (`ETA_MIN_SAMPLES_SECONDS = 5`): suppressed until the **current stage** has been running ≥5s AND the stage has a known total (`isEtaReady`) — an indeterminate stage (CLASSIFY/SCORE/WRITE, `total=0`) never shows an ETA, since there's no "remaining" to estimate against. The 5s clock is tracked by a new `ScanState.stageStartedAt` timestamp, re-stamped only on a stage **transition** (`useAppStore.ts` `ingestScanEvent`) so a freshly-started stage never inherits the prior stage's settled throughput. Hidden entirely while not yet meaningful; only computed while `scan.status === "running"`.
- **Conditions / variants:** The "N files/sec" number formatting is not routed through the i18n catalog, and neither are the ETA duration text `formatEta` builds (`~2m 05s`) and the "Running…" fallback shown for an empty stage name. **The rest of the panel is (copy audit SC4):** the `ETA …` label (`web.scan.eta_label`), the Cancel button (`web.scan.cancel`), the progress-bar and log `aria-label`s (`web.scan.progress_aria` / `log_aria`), and the indeterminate-stage count line, which now picks its noun per count (`web.scan.files_found_singular` / `_plural`) instead of rendering "1 files found" for the first file. The throughput and ETA rows are each suppressed independently: the throughput hides when the rate is 0, and the ETA also hides until the 5 s gate passes and whenever the stage has no known total. Both are derived in the same render from the same rate, so a rate of 0 hides both.
- **Cancel button:** the same running-state panel renders a **Cancel** button (testid `scan-cancel-button`, bottom-right of the panel) whenever `scan.status === "running"` — [frontend/src/components/scan/ScanProgress.tsx](../frontend/src/components/scan/ScanProgress.tsx). Leaving the page mid-scan is the other cancel path — see [Main window — close-during-scan confirm](#main-window--close-during-scan-confirm). Clicking it calls `store.cancelScan()` → `POST /api/scan/{task_id}/cancel` (`cancel_scan` in [app/web/routes/scan.py](../app/web/routes/scan.py)), which sets the cooperative `cancel_token` the scan worker polls (returns 404 for an unknown task, 409 if the task is already terminal). The scan pipeline checks the token between stages and — critically — **before the WRITE stage**, so a cancelled scan never writes its output `.db` (`scan_runner.py` logs "Scan cancelled." and returns before the manifest write). On the SSE side this lands as `scan.status = 'cancelled'`; `ScanDialog` reacts by closing itself and calling `resetScan()` (so reopening the dialog re-arms cleanly rather than showing stale sources) — no manifest auto-loads, since `loadManifest` is only invoked on the `'finished'` SSE transition, never on `'cancelled'`. The button only exists while running — it unmounts the instant the panel does, so there is no cancel affordance once a scan is idle/finished/failed.
- **Related:** See also [Scan dialog — stage / throughput / ETA progress (#424)](#scan-dialog--stage--throughput--eta-progress-424); [#740](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/740); vitest [`frontend/src/lib/scanProgress.test.ts`](../frontend/src/lib/scanProgress.test.ts) (pure helpers) + [`frontend/src/components/scan/ScanProgress.test.tsx`](../frontend/src/components/scan/ScanProgress.test.tsx) (rendering, incl. the unmapped-stage fallback); cancel button + endpoint: [app/web/routes/scan.py](../app/web/routes/scan.py); web scenario [`qa/web/scenarios/s03_cancel_scan.py`](../qa/web/scenarios/s03_cancel_scan.py) (two samples — HASH-stage and WALK-stage cancel. Both assert a clean dialog close; Sample 1 also asserts the app is left with no rows, and Sample 2 asserts the output manifest was never written).
- **Last verified:** 2026-07-03 (#740 — initial web port of throughput/ETA/localized stages); 2026-07-22 (web-docs-drift backfill — Cancel button documented, previously only a one-clause mention under Main window — close-during-scan confirm)

### Web — scan SSE connection-drop watchdog (#661)

- **Entry point:** `useScanSSE` ([frontend/src/hooks/useScanSSE.ts](../frontend/src/hooks/useScanSSE.ts)), mounted once in `App` for the active scan's `taskId`.
- **Behaviour:** While a scan streams, a client-side watchdog (90s) surfaces a `failed`-status error — *"Lost connection to the scanner. The scan may still be running on the server — reopen Scan to check the result."* — if the SSE stream goes fully silent, so a dead server no longer leaves the scan dialog frozen with no feedback (the error renders via the same `failed`-status surface as a real scan failure; the dialog stays open). The watchdog is reset by ANY incoming event, including a new **named `ping` keepalive** the backend emits every ~30s on an idle stream ([app/web/routes/scan.py](../app/web/routes/scan.py) `_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_S`). This replaces the previous keepalive *comment*: SSE comments keep the socket warm but fire NO JS handler, so the client could not distinguish "healthy but quiet" (a slow NAS or a big file — legitimately many seconds between progress events) from "server dead". The `ping` yields from the async event loop, decoupled from the scan thread, so it keeps flowing while the server lives regardless of scan speed; it carries no `id`, leaving Last-Event-ID resume untouched. `onerror` is still left to native auto-reconnect (a transient blip recovers via Last-Event-ID replay — see [#687 web parity](#execute-action--partial-execution-via-execute-selected)); only a drop that outlasts the watchdog surfaces the error. The message says the scan *may still be running* because the backend scan runs on a server thread independent of the browser's connection (`disconnect ≠ cancel`).
- **Related:** [#661](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/661). Bug 2 (PR #761) fixed the sibling lifecycle bug — scan status was not reset to `idle` after a scan, so reopening the dialog showed stale sources. Tests: backend [`tests/test_web_scan_routes.py`](../tests/test_web_scan_routes.py) `test_sse_idle_stream_emits_named_ping_keepalive` (named ping on an idle stream, observed on the wire) + frontend [`frontend/src/hooks/useScanSSE.test.ts`](../frontend/src/hooks/useScanSSE.test.ts) (fake-timer watchdog: fires after silence, rearms on ping, rearms on a real event, disarms on a terminal event). No live qa scenario — a true server-drop cannot be automated in the shared-server web-scenario batch without breaking co-tenant scenarios; the mechanism is deterministically unit-tested on both ends (server emits `ping` ↔ client watchdog consumes it).
- **Last verified:** 2026-07-05 (#661 — named ping + client connection-drop watchdog).

### Web — scan progress, skips and errors in the app log

- **Entry point:** `SseScanBus.log` and the scan-thread body `_run_scan` in [app/web/routes/scan.py](../app/web/routes/scan.py); the rotating file sink is `init_logging` in [infrastructure/logging.py](../infrastructure/logging.py) (`<home>\AppData\Local\PhotoManager\logs\app_<YYYYMMDD>.log`, 10 MB rotation, 10-day retention), set up by [launcher.py](../launcher.py).
- **Trigger:** Automatic during every web scan.
- **Behaviour:** The web backend is the writer: every line the scan dialog's progress log shows is also written to the app log at `INFO`, prefixed `scan: `, so after the dialog closes the file still records what was walked, the `Hashed N/M` progress, the `Skipped N unreadable file(s)` block (each listed path with its `[ExceptionType: message]`), the manifest summary and `Done.`. A pipeline exception that aborts the scan is logged at `ERROR` as `Scan pipeline failed: <message>` **with its traceback**, then reaches the dialog as the usual `failed` event. A clean cancel is logged by the pipeline itself as a `WARNING` naming the stage it stopped in. SSE delivery to the dialog is unchanged.
- **Conditions / variants:** Same granularity as the desktop client this replaces: progress is the pipeline's own cadence (`Hashed N/M` every 100 files plus the final count), never one line per file; the ≈1/s `stage` throughput events are not mirrored; the skip block lists the first 10 unreadable files, then `… and N more`, exactly as the dialog does. The sink is `enqueue=True`, so a log write never blocks the scan thread on disk I/O.
- **Related:** [#886](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/886) — restores [#49](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/49)'s guarantee, which the desktop `ScanWorker._emit` / `ScanWorker.run` provided until the Qt client was removed by #646. Pinned by `tests/test_web_scan_bus.py::TestScanAppLogMirror` (progress + skip lines mirrored in order and at the pipeline's cadence; a real `FileExistsError` from a mistyped output path logged with its traceback).
- **Last verified:** 2026-09-29 (#886)

### Web — desktop shell (pywebview launcher)

- **Entry point:** [launcher.py](../launcher.py) (repo root) — the only entry point; `run.bat` and the packaged exe both call it.
- **Trigger:** Any launch. Since #646 there is no second client to dispatch to, so the web shell boots unconditionally; `PHOTO_MANAGER_WEB` is accepted and ignored so an existing shell profile keeps working.
- **Behaviour:** `launcher.py` starts the FastAPI app under uvicorn on `127.0.0.1:8765` (override via `PHOTO_MANAGER_WEB_PORT`) in a daemon thread, waits for `GET /api/health` to return 200, verifies the Edge WebView2 runtime is installed, then opens a native 1280×800 pywebview window titled **Photo Manager** pointed at the loopback URL. If WebView2 is missing it raises a clear `RuntimeError` naming the install URL rather than showing a silent blank window. Closing the window signals uvicorn's graceful shutdown (so `app.web.main`'s lifespan drain runs) and exits.
- **Conditions / variants:** The WebView2 runtime check is Windows-only (a no-op on other platforms, which use a different pywebview backend). The server binds loopback only (never `0.0.0.0`), so the API is unreachable off-box. The SPA is served from `frontend/dist` (built via `npm run build`); in a dev checkout a missing dist just loads the bare API root, but a FROZEN build (PyInstaller) raises a loud `RuntimeError` at startup instead — a bundle without its SPA is a broken artifact (#772). In the frozen windowed exe (no console), startup-fatal `RuntimeError`s (missing WebView2, unhealthy server, missing bundled SPA) are additionally surfaced in a native message box before the non-zero exit. `PHOTO_MANAGER_WEB_SMOKE` (truthy) selects a server-only CI smoke: boot uvicorn, poll `/api/health`, shut down, exit 0/1 — no window, takes precedence over the window path.
- **Related:** [launcher.py](../launcher.py); [tests/test_launcher.py](../tests/test_launcher.py); [app/web/main.py](../app/web/main.py) (uvicorn factory + `/api/health`); [tests/test_web_spa_mount.py](../tests/test_web_spa_mount.py); epic [#644](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/644) (Phase 2 — pywebview shell). PyInstaller packaging shipped in #772: [pyinstaller.spec](../pyinstaller.spec) entry = `launcher.py`, bundles `frontend/dist`, pywebview/pythonnet + uvicorn hiddenimports; [release.yml](../.github/workflows/release.yml) builds the frontend, asserts the bundled SPA inside `_internal`, runs the server-only web smoke, and ships the third-party notices in the zip. The `photo-manager-web.bat` double-click helper was dropped in #646-C: it existed only to `set PHOTO_MANAGER_WEB=1`, so after the cutover it was a second double-click target doing exactly what `photo-manager.exe` does.
- **Last verified:** 2026-07-11 (#772 — `tests/test_launcher.py` + `tests/test_web_spa_mount.py` PASS locally; the packaged-exe path is proven by the release workflow's bundle checks + web smoke on the next test tag; the native WebView2 window remains a manual Windows smoke).

### Web — settings dialog

- **Entry point:** Toolbar `main-settings-button` ("Settings") — [frontend/src/App.tsx:346-352](../frontend/src/App.tsx#L346) — `onClick` calls `setSettingsOpen(true)`, mounting `SettingsDialog` ([frontend/src/components/SettingsDialog.tsx](../frontend/src/components/SettingsDialog.tsx)). Backed by `GET`/`PATCH /api/settings` ([app/web/routes/settings.py](../app/web/routes/settings.py)).
- **Trigger:** User clicks the toolbar Settings button (always enabled, no manifest-loaded gate — unlike Execute/Set Action). Opening the dialog fires `loadSettings()`, which re-fetches current values every time (no stale cache across opens).
- **Behaviour:** A minimal dialog (testid `settings-dialog`) exposing exactly three of the allowlisted settings keys as editable controls (every other `settings.json` key is owned by its own feature — see Conditions — or edited by hand in the file):
  - **`ui.prune_singletons`** (testid `settings-prune-select`) — a 3-option `<select>` (Ask each time / Always prune / Never prune), the enum the [Singleton-prune offer](#singleton-prune-offer-after-destructive-ops-426) reads. Visible label: **"Prune singletons"**.
  - **`ui.scan_dialog.autotune_read_knee`** — a checkbox, the same key the [Scan dialog — read-knee autotune opt-out](#scan-dialog--read-knee-autotune-opt-out) reads. Visible label: **"Auto-tune reader concurrency (experimental)"** — the same words the Scan dialog's Advanced-settings row uses, so one toggle reads the same in both places.
  - **`sorting.defaults`** — edited as a raw JSON textarea (its shape is not fixed, so the dialog round-trips it through `JSON.parse`/`JSON.stringify` rather than dedicated fields); an unparsable edit shows an inline `role="alert"` error and blocks Save without discarding the typed text. Visible label: **"Sorting defaults"**; the textarea is hinted with `[]` (this key's actual default).
  - **Labels carry no raw config keys (copy audit ST1/ST2).** All three labels used to end in the settings-file key — "Prune singletons **(ui.prune_singletons)**" — and the JSON textarea's placeholder was the literal word `null`, which reads as a bug rather than a hint. Each key now lives in the row's hover description (`web.settings.*_desc` on the label's `title`), so it stays discoverable without being the label.
  - **Save** (testid `settings-save-button`, disabled while `settings.loading`) calls `saveSettings(updates)` → `PATCH /api/settings` with all three keys, then closes the dialog. **Cancel** (testid `settings-cancel-button`) closes without saving — local edits are discarded, since the three fields are re-derived from `settings.values` fresh on every open.
- **Conditions / variants:** `PATCH /api/settings` validates every key against a server-side allowlist (`_WEB_SETTINGS_KEYS` in [app/web/routes/settings.py](../app/web/routes/settings.py)) BEFORE applying any write — a key outside the allowlist 400s the whole request; the allowlist is much broader than what this dialog exposes (it also covers `ui.locale`, `sources.list`/`sources.output`, `scan.exif_workers`, and `ui.action_dialog.recent_patterns`, each read/written by its own feature — [Language switch](#language-switch) § Web variant, [Web — ScanDialog source/output persistence](#web--scandialog-sourceoutput-persistence), [Web — Action dialog](#web--action-dialog-set-action-by-field) § Recent patterns — rather than through this dialog). A non-dict intermediate node in a dotted-key write (`#658`) surfaces as a 422, not a silent clobber. **Which file is edited** is decided in one place — `infrastructure/settings.py`'s `resolve_settings_path()` / `load_settings()`, the single resolver every caller shares since [#882](https://github.com/jackal998/photo-manager/issues/882): `PHOTO_MANAGER_HOME` if set (relative values resolved against the app root), else the app root itself — which is the directory holding `photo-manager.exe` in a packaged build and the repo root from source. In the installer that means `<install folder>\settings.json`, deliberately NOT `_internal\settings.json`: `_internal\` is replaced wholesale by every upgrade, so settings resolved there were lost on each new release.
- **Related:** `frontend/src/lib/prune.ts` (`PrunePref` enum + `normalizePrunePref`); backend allowlist + validation tests in [tests/test_web_settings_routes.py](../tests/test_web_settings_routes.py) (covers the `_WEB_SETTINGS_KEYS` allowlist and round-trips `sorting.defaults`/`ui.prune_singletons`); no dedicated qa/web scenario name was found citing `settings-dialog` at audit time — the three keys are exercised indirectly via the features that consume them ([Singleton-prune](#singleton-prune-offer-after-destructive-ops-426), [Scan dialog — read-knee autotune opt-out](#scan-dialog--read-knee-autotune-opt-out)).
- **Last verified:** 2026-07-22 (web-docs-drift backfill — initial documentation of this surface; no prior features.md entry existed)
